`POST /api/v1/m16/optical/price/preview`（API-09）取定金比例与金额；4) **本模块不做任何金额计算**，
只做透传与合并；5) 任一上游失败返回对应错误码（M01-08 异常 → `"1006004"`；M16 异常 → 降级返回
零售价并标注 `degraded: true`）；6) 结果不落库。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否。

#### API-10 提交订单

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/PO202610310021/submit
Idempotency-Key: 9b8c7d6e-5f4a-3b2c-1d0e-9f8a7b6c5d4e
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310021",
    "sales_order_no": "SO202610310021",
    "status": "SUBMITTED",
    "sales_order_status": "DRAFT",
    "total_amount_snap": 2593.80,
    "deposit_amount_snap": 778.14,
    "is_idempotent": false,
    "submitted_at": "2026-10-31T15:22:45+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 仅 `DRAFT` 可提交，否则 `"1006019"`；2) 若已有 `sales_order_no`（M16 反建场景）
则跳过建单，直接置 `SUBMITTED`（幂等路径 B）；3) 调 M16-04 `POST /internal/v1/m16/optical/order`
（API-24），载荷含 `source = 'M10'`、`source_id = m10_order.id`、明细、验光单号；4) M16 返回
`order_no` 后回写 `sales_order_no` / `sales_order_id` / 金额快照；5) **唯一索引冲突处理**：若
`uk_m10_order_sales` 冲突（并发下被 P-13 抢先创建），捕获 `IntegrityError` → 查询已存在档案 →
若 `order_no` 不同则返回 `"1006007"`（数据异常需人工介入），若相同则幂等返回；6) 写状态日志
（`DRAFT → SUBMITTED`，`action_source = 'USER'`）；7) M16 调用失败返回 `"1006005"`，订单保持
`DRAFT` 可重试；8) 发布 `m10.order.created` 事件。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-11 光度可做性批量校验

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/spec-check
```

```json
{
  "items": [
    {"product_code": "LN0158", "sphere": -4.25, "cylinder": -0.75, "axis": 180},
    {"product_code": "LN0160", "sphere": -8.50, "cylinder": -2.75, "axis": 5}
  ]
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {
        "product_code": "LN0158",
        "feasible": true,
        "delivery_days": 5,
        "price_add": 0.00,
        "message": "现货可做"
      },
      {
        "product_code": "LN0160",
        "feasible": false,
        "delivery_days": null,
        "price_add": null,
        "message": "该镜片柱镜最大支持 -2.00D，请更换镜片或调整处方"
      }
    ],
    "all_feasible": false
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 代理 M01-08 `POST /internal/v1/optical/specs/batch-lookup`（API-21），本模块
**不维护**光度矩阵；2) 结果缓存到 Redis（`m10:order:spec:{product_code}:{sphere}:{cylinder}`，
TTL 30 分钟，受 `mdm.optical_spec.changed` 事件失效）；3) 任一不可做时 `all_feasible = false`，
前端行标红；4) M01-08 不可用时返回 `"1006004"` 并标注 `degraded: true`（放行但提示人工核对）。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否。

#### API-12 催加工

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/labor-urge
```

```json
{
  "order_nos": ["PO202610210008"],
  "urge_reason": "顾客来电询问进度，已超预计到货日 5 天"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "success_count": 1,
    "failed": [],
    "urged_at": "2026-11-01T10:20:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验订单处于 `IN_PRODUCTION`，否则 `"1006019"`；2) 调 M10-07
`POST /internal/v1/m10/lab/jobs/{lab_job_no}/urge`（催工）并写站内消息给加工师；3) 写审计日志；
4) 同一订单 2 小时内重复催工只记录不重复推送（防骚扰）；5) 部分失败时返回
`failed` 列表与原因，不全量失败。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否。

#### API-13 取消订单

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/PO202610310021/cancel
```

```json
{
  "version": 7,
  "cancel_type": "CUSTOMER_CANCEL",
  "cancel_reason": "顾客临时决定不配了，要求取消本次订单"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310021",
    "status": "CANCELLED",
    "sales_order_status": "CANCELLED",
    "cancelled_at": "2026-11-01T11:00:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 仅 `DRAFT` / `SUBMITTED`（且财务态 `DRAFT`）可直接取消；2) `PAID` 及以后必须
先由 M16 走退费（`"1006027"`）；3) `IN_PRODUCTION` 必须走质检退货（`"1006040"`）；4) 原因 ≥ 10 字
（`"1006036"` 复用改单原因的校验口径，此处返回 `"1006003"` 参数错误）；5) 乐观锁校验；6) 已建销售单
时先调 M16-04 `POST /api/v1/m16/optical/orders/{id}/cancel`（API-05）作废销售单；7) 写状态日志
（`action_source = 'USER'`，`reason` 必填）；8) 释放加工预占（调 M10-07 `P-20`）；9) 发布
`m10.order.cancelled` 事件。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否。

#### API-14 手动派工

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/PO202610310021/dispatch-lab
```

```json
{
  "lab_priority": "NORMAL",
  "expected_complete_date": "2026-11-05",
  "remark": ""
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310021",
    "status": "IN_PRODUCTION",
    "lab_job_no": "JG202611040008",
    "dispatched_at": "2026-10-31T15:41:08+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 仅 `PAID` 可派工（`"1006019"`）；2) `need_processing = FALSE` 的订单不允许派工，
提示走 API-15；3) 调 M10-07 `POST /internal/v1/m10/lab/on-order-paid`（P-17），载荷含
`order_no`、明细工序需求、光度参数、`lab_priority`；4) 成功返回 `lab_job_no` 后回写
`m10_order.lab_job_no` / `lab_status = 'PENDING'` / `status = 'IN_PRODUCTION'`；5) 幂等：若已有
`lab_job_no` 且加工单未取消，直接返回既有单号（`is_idempotent: true`）；6) M10-07 异常返回
`"1006006"`，订单停在 `PAID` 可重试；7) 写状态日志；8) 调 M16-04 API-26 回写加工状态。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-15 无需加工订单标记到货

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/PO202610310019/mark-ready
```

```json
{
  "arrived_at": "2026-10-31T16:00:00+08:00",
  "remark": "纯现货配件订单，柜台直接交付"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310019",
    "status": "READY",
    "delivery_status": "READY_TO_PICK",
    "ready_at": "2026-10-31T16:00:00+08:00",
    "pickup_deadline": "2026-11-30"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 仅 `need_processing = FALSE` 的订单可调用，否则提示走 API-14 / 等加工事件
（`"1006019"`）；2) 仅 `PAID` 可标记到货；3) 计算 `pickup_deadline = arrived_at::date +
M10.ORDER.PICKUP_KEEP_DAYS`；4) 明细全部置 `arrive_status = 'NOT_REQUIRED'`；
5) 写状态日志（`PAID → READY`）；6) 发布 `m10.order.ready` 事件触发到货通知。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-16 主动同步 M16 财务态

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/PO202610310021/sync-sales
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310021",
    "sales_order_status": "PAID",
    "total_amount_snap": 2593.80,
    "paid_amount_snap": 2593.80,
    "refund_amount_snap": 0.00,
    "changed": false,
    "synced_at": "2026-11-01T09:00:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 调 M16-04 `GET /internal/v1/m16/optical/order/{order_no}`（API-25）拉取销售单；
2) 对比本地快照，仅在有变化时更新 `sales_order_status` / 金额快照 / `sales_status_synced_at`；
3) 若财务态变化触发业务态迁移（如 `PAID` → `paid_amount_snap > 0` → `status = 'PAID'`），走
状态机校验与状态日志；4) 该接口用于事件丢失的人工补偿，也供每日对账任务调用；
5) M16 不可用时返回 `"1006005"` 不修改任何数据。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-17 改单试算

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/PO202610310021/amend/preview
```

```json
{
  "amend_type": "CHANGE_LENS",
  "changes": [
    {"op": "UPDATE", "line_no": 2, "field": "product_code", "after": "LN0160"},
    {"op": "UPDATE", "line_no": 3, "field": "product_code", "after": "LN0160"},
    {"op": "UPDATE", "line_no": 3, "field": "axis", "after": 180}
  ]
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310021",
    "amend_stage": "IN_PRODUCTION",
    "is_restricted": true,
    "before_amount_snap": 2593.80,
    "after_amount_snap": 2993.80,
    "diff_amount_snap": 400.00,
    "diff_direction": "CHARGE",
    "need_approval": true,
    "approval_reason": "改单阶段为加工中，需主管审批",
    "lab_impact": "SCRAP",
    "lab_impact_text": "已加工镜片 2 片需报废重做",
    "affected_lines": [2, 3],
    "warnings": [
      "本次改单涉及已加工镜片，将产生报废损失，请确认后再提交"
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验订单非终态（`"1006035"`）；2) 判定 `amend_stage`：按 `status` 映射
（`DRAFT`/`SUBMITTED`→`BEFORE_PAID`，`PAID`→`AFTER_PAID`，`IN_PRODUCTION`→`IN_PRODUCTION`，
`READY`→`READY`）；3) 计算 `lab_impact`（见 3.3 改单类型与加工影响矩阵）；4) 把变更项应用成「改后
明细」，调 M16-04 API-09 试算改后总额；5) 计算 `diff_amount_snap = after - before` 与方向；
6) 判定 `need_approval`（`|diff| >= 500` 或加工中/已到货）；7) **不落库**，仅返回预览结果；
8) 试算失败时返回上游错误码，不返回半截数据。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否。

#### API-18 提交改单

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/PO202610310021/amend
Idempotency-Key: 3c2d1e0f-9a8b-7c6d-5e4f-3a2b1c0d9e8f
```

```json
{
  "version": 7,
  "amend_type": "CHANGE_LENS",
  "amend_reason": "顾客反映右眼戴镜头晕，医生确认需调整散光轴位并更换镜片型号",
  "changes": [
    {"op": "UPDATE", "line_no": 2, "field": "product_code", "before": "LN0158", "after": "LN0160"},
    {"op": "UPDATE", "line_no": 3, "field": "product_code", "before": "LN0158", "after": "LN0160"},
    {"op": "UPDATE", "line_no": 3, "field": "axis", "before": 175, "after": 180}
  ],
  "accept_rework": true,
  "rework_confirm_no": "QC202611040002",
  "remark": ""
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310021",
    "amend_no": "GD202611010002",
    "amend_seq": 2,
    "amend_stage": "IN_PRODUCTION",
    "is_restricted": true,
    "before_amount_snap": 2593.80,
    "after_amount_snap": 2993.80,
    "diff_amount_snap": 400.00,
    "diff_direction": "CHARGE",
    "diff_settle_status": "PENDING",
    "lab_impact": "SCRAP",
    "lab_job_no_new": "JG202611010009",
    "need_approval": true,
    "approval_status": "PENDING",
    "approval_no": "SP202611010003"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等；2) 订单非终态（`"1006035"`）；3) 原因 ≥ 10 字
（`"1006036"`）；4) 改单次数上限 20（`"1006041"`）；5) 加工中/已到货阶段必须 `accept_rework = true`
且填 `rework_confirm_no`，否则 `"1006040"`；6) 乐观锁校验（`40900`）；7) 重跑 API-17 的试算逻辑，
以服务端结果为准；8) 生成 `amend_no`（GD 前缀），写 `m10_order_amend`（含
`change_items` / `before_snapshot` / `after_snapshot`）；9) 应用变更到 `m10_order_item` 与
`m10_order` 金额快照；10) `lab_impact` 非 `NONE` 时调 M10-07 作废原加工单并重派（`REASSIGN` /
`SCRAP` / `REWORK`），回写 `lab_job_no_new`；11) `need_approval = true` 时调 M20 发起审批并置
`approval_status = 'PENDING'`；12) `diff != 0` 时置 `diff_settle_status = 'PENDING'`，等 M16
补收/退差；13) 写状态日志（`action_code = 'AMEND_ORDER'`）；14) 发布 `m10.order.amended` 事件。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-19 改单记录列表

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/orders/PO202610310021/amends
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310021",
    "items": [
      {
        "amend_no": "GD202610310001",
        "amend_seq": 1,
        "amend_type": "ADD_ACCESSORY",
        "amend_reason": "顾客要求加购镜盒与镜布",
        "amend_stage": "AFTER_PAID",
        "is_restricted": false,
        "before_amount_snap": 2563.80,
        "after_amount_snap": 2593.80,
        "diff_amount_snap": 30.00,
        "diff_direction": "CHARGE",
        "diff_settle_status": "CHARGED",
        "diff_settlement_no": "BL202610310021",
        "lab_impact": "NONE",
        "need_approval": false,
        "amend_by_name": "张敏",
        "amended_at": "2026-10-31T16:10:00+08:00"
      }
    ],
    "total": 3,
    "pending_diff_amount": 0.00
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `order_id` 查 `m10_order_amend`（`idx_m10_order_amend_order`）；2) 按
`amend_seq ASC` 排序；3) `diff_direction` 由 `diff_amount_snap` 正负推导；4) 汇总
`pending_diff_amount`（`diff_settle_status = 'PENDING'` 合计）供 P04 角标使用。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否。

#### API-20 改单审批结果回调

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/amend/GD202611010002/approve
```

```json
{
  "approval_no": "SP202611010003",
  "approval_status": "APPROVED",
  "approved_by": 2001,
  "approved_at": "2026-11-01T11:30:00+08:00",
  "opinion": "同意，属于处方调整合理需求"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "amend_no": "GD202611010002",
    "approval_status": "APPROVED",
    "applied": true,
    "order_no": "PO202610310021"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 内部服务调用（M20 审批流回调），权限写「内部服务」，需校验来源 IP 白名单 +
内网签名；2) 按 `amend_no` 查记录，不存在返回 `"40400"`；3) `APPROVED` 时把改动生效
（`is_restricted` 改为 false 并触发加工重派）；4) `REJECTED` 时回滚明细到 `before_snapshot`；
5) 幂等：重复回调同一 `approval_no` 且状态相同直接返回成功；6) 写状态日志。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是（按 `approval_no`）。

#### API-21 改单差额结算回填

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/amend/GD202611010002/diff-settle
```

```json
{
  "diff_settle_status": "CHARGED",
  "diff_settlement_no": "BL202611010005",
  "diff_settled_at": "2026-11-01T14:00:00+08:00",
  "remark": "顾客现场补缴差额 400 元"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "amend_no": "GD202611010002",
    "diff_settle_status": "CHARGED",
    "diff_settlement_no": "BL202611010005",
    "order_no": "PO202610310021"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `CHARGED` 必须有 `diff_settlement_no`，`REFUNDED` 必须有 `diff_refund_no`
（否则 `"40001"`）；2) 与数据库 `ck_m10_order_amend_charged` / `ck_m10_order_amend_refunded` 双重
保护；3) 差额结清后该改单不再阻断交付（解除 `"1006037"`）；4) 全部改单结清后订单
`pending_diff_amount = 0`；5) 幂等：同状态重复回填直接返回成功。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。


#### API-22 补单

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/supplement
Idempotency-Key: 5e4d3c2b-1a0f-9e8d-7c6b-5a4f3e2d1c0b
```

```json
{
  "parent_order_no": "PO202608150003",
  "supplement_reason": "SINGLE_EYE",
  "eye_side": "OD",
  "is_urgent": true,
  "items": [
    {
      "line_no": 1,
      "item_type": "LENS",
      "product_code": "LN0158",
      "quantity": 1,
      "unit": "片",
      "eye_side": "OD",
      "lens_type": "SINGLE_VISION",
      "supply_type": "CUSTOM",
      "sphere": -4.25,
      "cylinder": -0.75,
      "axis": 180,
      "pd_total": 64.00,
      "pd_right": 32.50,
      "pd_left": 31.50
    }
  ],
  "remark": "右眼镜片摔碎，单眼补配"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202611010022",
    "parent_order_no": "PO202608150003",
    "is_supplement": true,
    "supplement_reason": "SINGLE_EYE",
    "status": "DRAFT",
    "expected_ready_date": "2026-11-06",
    "is_urgent": true,
    "created_at": "2026-11-01T10:00:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验原订单存在（`"1006044"`）；2) 原订单必须已交付（`status = 'DELIVERED'`），
否则说明是改单场景应走 API-18（`"1006044"`）；3) 补单有效期内（`delivered_at` 距今 ≤
`M10.ORDER.SUPPLEMENT_MAX_DAYS`，默认 365 天，超期 `"1006044"`）；4) `SINGLE_EYE` 必须指定眼别
且不能为 `OU`（`"1006003"`）；5) 从原订单继承：`patient_id`、`customer_*`、`optometry_no`、
`prescription_no`、`store_id`；6) 新建订单 `create_path = 'M10_AMEND'`、`is_supplement = TRUE`、
`parent_order_id = 原单 id`；7) 光度沿用原订单对应眼别值（可由前端覆盖）；8) 生成 `order_no` 并写
主表与明细；9) 后续走标准提交（API-10）→ 派工 → 交付；10) 发布 `m10.order.created` 事件。

> **补单 ≠ 改单**：补单是**新建订单**（新销售单、新订单号、独立收款与交付），改单是**修改原订单**。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-23 取镜四道校验

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/orders/PO202610310021/delivery-check
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310021",
    "check_passed": false,
    "check_paid": true,
    "check_paid_detail": {
      "total_amount": 2593.80,
      "paid_amount": 2593.80,
      "sales_status": "PAID",
      "paid_full": true
    },
    "check_lab_done": true,
    "check_lab_detail": {
      "lab_job_no": "JG202611040008",
      "lab_status": "COMPLETED",
      "qc_result": "PASSED",
      "completed_at": "2026-11-04T08:50:00+08:00"
    },
    "check_all_arrived": false,
    "check_arrived_detail": {
      "total_lines": 5,
      "arrived_lines": 4,
      "not_required_lines": 1,
      "pending_lines": [
        {"line_no": 5, "product_name": "镜盒", "eye_side": "NA", "arrive_status": "PENDING"}
      ]
    },
    "check_customer_confirm": false,
    "check_confirm_detail": {"confirmed_count": 0, "required_count": 6},
    "check_failed_code": "1006023",
    "check_failed_message": "存在未到货商品，暂不可取镜",
    "confirm_required_items": [
      {"key": "rx_matched", "text": "已核对镜片度数与订单一致", "required": true},
      {"key": "frame_ok", "text": "已核对镜框外观无瑕疵", "required": true},
      {"key": "wear_ok", "text": "已确认佩戴舒适、无头晕", "required": true},
      {"key": "care_guide", "text": "已告知镜片清洁与保养要点", "required": true},
      {"key": "warranty", "text": "已告知质保范围与期限", "required": true},
      {"key": "followup", "text": "已告知复查时间", "required": true}
    ],
    "checked_at": "2026-11-04T09:20:00+08:00",
    "delivery_seq": 1
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `order_no` 取订单，不存在 `"1006001"`；2) **第一道**：`check_paid = (status IN
('READY','DELIVERED')) AND (sales_order_status IN ('PAID','DELIVERED') OR paid_amount_snap >=
total_amount_snap)`，失败 `"1006021"`；以 M16 财务态为**最终依据**，快照作辅助（事件可能延迟）；
3) **第二道**：`check_lab_done = (lab_status = 'COMPLETED') OR (need_processing = FALSE)`，失败
`"1006022"`；4) **第三道**：查 `m10_order_item` 全部行 `arrive_status IN ('ARRIVED','NOT_REQUIRED')`，
否则 `"1006023"` 并返回未到货行清单（走 `idx_m10_order_item_arrive`）；5) **第四道**：
`check_customer_confirm` 由前端勾选后传入，本接口返回需确认项清单与已确认数，全勾才为 true，
否则 `"1006024"`；6) 四条按顺序短路：`check_failed_code` 取**第一个失败项**的错误码；
7) 计算 `delivery_seq = COALESCE(MAX(delivery_seq),0)+1`；8) 本接口**只读不写**，不落库。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否。

#### API-24 创建取镜单

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/PO202610310021/deliveries
Idempotency-Key: 8a7b6c5d-4e3f-2a1b-0c9d-8e7f6a5b4c3d
```

```json
{
  "order_no": "PO202610310021",
  "is_partial": false,
  "deliver_method": "IN_STORE",
  "pickup_person_name": "王女士",
  "pickup_person_relation": "SELF",
  "items": [
    {
      "order_item_id": 990001,
      "delivered_qty": 1,
      "param_matched": true,
      "packaging_intact": true,
      "appearance_ok": true,
      "serial_no": "L20261018A",
      "batch_no": "L20261018"
    },
    {
      "order_item_id": 990002,
      "delivered_qty": 1,
      "param_matched": true,
      "packaging_intact": true,
      "appearance_ok": true,
      "serial_no": "L20261018B",
      "batch_no": "L20261018"
    }
  ],
  "remark": ""
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310021",
    "delivery_no": "QJ202611040006",
    "delivery_seq": 1,
    "is_partial": false,
    "delivery_status": "DRAFT",
    "check_passed": true,
    "item_count": 2,
    "warranty_card_no": "WK202611040006",
    "warranty_end_date": "2027-11-04"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等；2) 服务端**重新执行 API-23 四道校验**（不信任前端结论），
任一失败返回对应错误码；3) 订单状态必须 `READY`（`"1006020"`）；4) 校验改单差额全部结清
（`diff_settle_status <> 'PENDING'`），否则 `"1006037"`；5) 逐行校验：`order_item_id` 属于该订单
且 `is_delivered = FALSE`；`delivered_qty <= quantity - delivered_qty`；`param_matched = FALSE` 时
`param_diff` 必须非空（`"1006032"`）；6) **强制参数核对**：`items` 必须覆盖本次所有可交付行，
缺行返回 `"1006031"`；7) 生成 `delivery_no`（QJ）与 `warranty_card_no`（WK）；8) 计算
`is_partial`（本次交付后是否还有未交付行）与 `delivery_seq`；9) 写 `m10_delivery`（四道校验布尔 +
四个 JSONB 详情快照）与 `m10_delivery_item`；10) 更新 `m10_order_item` 的
`delivered_qty` / `delivered_in_id` / `is_delivered` / `serial_no`；11) `uk_m10_delivery_seq` 冲突
返回 `"1006038"`；12) 写状态日志（`action_code = 'DELIVER_DRAFT'`）。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-25 取镜签字与举证上传并完成交付

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/deliveries/QJ202611040006/sign
Idempotency-Key: 2b1a0c9d-8e7f-6a5b-4c3d-2e1f0a9b8c7d
```

```json
{
  "delivery_no": "QJ202611040006",
  "version": 0,
  "sign_type": "ELECTRONIC",
  "signature_url": "m10/signature/2026/11/04/QJ202611040006.png",
  "signer_name": "王女士",
  "photo_package_url": "m10/photo/2026/11/04/QJ202611040006_pkg.jpg",
  "photo_product_url": "m10/photo/2026/11/04/QJ202611040006_prd.jpg",
  "care_guide_given": true,
  "confirm_note": "顾客确认佩戴舒适"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310021",
    "delivery_no": "QJ202611040006",
    "delivery_status": "COMPLETED",
    "order_status": "DELIVERED",
    "delivery_status_field": "DELIVERED",
    "delivered_at": "2026-11-04T10:15:33+08:00",
    "signature_hash": "3f2a7b9c1d8e4f6a0b5c2d9e7f1a3b4c5d6e8f0a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8c9d0e1f2a",
    "warranty_card_no": "WK202611040006",
    "warranty_start_date": "2026-11-04",
    "warranty_end_date": "2027-11-04",
    "followup_plan_no": "FC202611040002",
    "notified_systems": ["M16-04", "M10-09", "M10-08", "M03-03"]
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等；2) 校验签字（`sign_type` 不能 NONE，电子签名必须有
`signature_url`，纸质必须有 `paper_scan_url`，否则 `"1006025"`）；3) 强制交付举证：
`photo_package_url` 必填（`M10.ORDER.PHOTO_REQUIRED = TRUE` 时），否则 `"1006026"`；
4) 代取必须 `pickup_person_id_card`（`"1006028"`）；快递必须有公司与单号（`"1006029"`）；
5) 服务端从 OSS 读取签名文件计算 SHA-256 存 `signature_hash`，照片组计算整体 `photo_hash`；
6) 置 `m10_delivery.delivery_status = 'COMPLETED'`、`signed_at`、`delivered_at`、`delivered_by`；
7) 若本次为完整交付（`is_partial = FALSE`）：订单 `status = 'DELIVERED'`、
`delivery_status = 'DELIVERED'`、`delivered_at`；计算质保起止
（`warranty_start_date = delivered_at::date`，`warranty_end_date = +warranty_months`，月末对齐）
并写 `warranty_card_no`；若为部分交付则订单保持 `READY`；
8) 写 `m10_warranty`（`record_type = 'WARRANTY_CARD'`）；9) 写状态日志（`action_code =
'DELIVER_ORDER'`，`action_source = 'USER'`）；10) 内部调用 **P-15 on-delivered** 通知 M16-04 /
M10-09 / M10-08 / M03-03，任一失败进重试队列不阻断交付完成；11) 发布 `m10.order.delivered` 事件。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-28 发起维修申请

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/warranty/repair
Idempotency-Key: 1a2b3c4d-5e6f-7a8b-9c0d-1e2f3a4b5c6d
```

```json
{
  "order_no": "PO202610310021",
  "order_item_id": 990001,
  "fault_type": "SCRATCH",
  "fault_desc": "右眼镜片中央有 3mm 划痕，影响夜间视物",
  "fault_eye_side": "OD",
  "fault_images": ["m10/fault/2026/11/05/WK202611050001_1.jpg"],
  "customer_request": "希望免费更换镜片"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "warranty_no": "WK202611050001",
    "order_no": "PO202610310021",
    "record_type": "REPAIR",
    "fault_type": "SCRATCH",
    "in_warranty": true,
    "warranty_end_date": "2027-11-04",
    "remain_days": 364,
    "judge_result": "PENDING",
    "created_at": "2026-11-05T10:20:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 订单必须 `DELIVERED`（`"1006030"`）；2) 必须存在 `WARRANTY_CARD` 记录
（`"1006043"`）；3) 故障描述 ≥ 5 字（`"1006033"`）；4) 系统自动判定 `in_warranty = (受理日 <=
warranty_end_date)` 与 `remain_days`，供页面展示；5) 生成 `warranty_no`（WK），写
`m10_warranty`（`record_type = 'REPAIR'`、`judge_result = 'PENDING'`、`repair_status = 'PENDING'`）；
6) 订单 `repair_count` +1；7) 发布 `m10.warranty.repair.created` 事件供 M22 质量分析。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-29 质保判定

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/warranty/WK202611050001/judge
```

```json
{
  "warranty_no": "WK202611050001",
  "is_quality_issue": true,
  "judge_result": "FREE_REPAIR",
  "judge_basis": "镜片中央区域密集细微划痕，无外力击打痕迹，符合镀膜附着力不足导致的质量问题",
  "is_free": true,
  "need_redeliver": true
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "warranty_no": "WK202611050001",
    "judge_result": "FREE_REPAIR",
    "in_warranty": true,
    "is_free": true,
    "fee_amount_snap": 0.00,
    "need_approval": false,
    "repair_status": "PENDING",
    "need_redeliver": true,
    "judge_at": "2026-11-05T10:35:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 判定依据 ≥ 10 字（`"1006034"`）；2) 判定自洽校验：`FREE_REPAIR` / `FREE_REPLACE`
必须 `in_warranty = TRUE` 且 `is_free = TRUE`；`PAID_REPAIR` 必须 `is_free = FALSE` 且有
`charge_item_code` 与 `fee_amount`（缺则 `"1006048"`）；矛盾返回 `"1006045"`；
3) 与数据库 `ck_m10_warranty_in_warranty` 双重保护；4) `FREE_REPLACE` 或超保仍免费 →
`need_approval = TRUE`，调 M20 发起审批；5) 付费维修时调 M16-04 API-24 生成维修销售单
（`source = 'M10_REPAIR'`, `source_id = m10_warranty.id`），由 M16 收银；
6) 写 `judge_by` / `judge_at` / `repair_status`；7) 状态机校验：不可对已判定记录重复判定
（`"1006019"`，需先作废）。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-30 维修完成

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/warranty/WK202611050001/complete
```

```json
{
  "warranty_no": "WK202611050001",
  "repair_type": "RELENS",
  "repair_operator_id": 6001,
  "need_redeliver": true,
  "repair_remark": "更换右眼镜片，参数 -4.25D/-0.75D×180°，已核对无误"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "warranty_no": "WK202611050001",
    "repair_status": "REPAIRED",
    "repair_end_at": "2026-11-07T15:00:00+08:00",
    "repair_days": 2,
    "need_redeliver": true,
    "redeliver_delivery_no": "QJ202611070002",
    "order_status": "READY",
    "followup_plan_no": null
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 必须有 `repair_operator_id`，否则 `"1006049"`；2) 仅 `PENDING` / `REPAIRING` /
`OUTSOURCED` 可完成，否则 `"1006019"`；3) 写 `repair_end_at`、`repair_status = 'REPAIRED'`、
计算 `repair_days`；4) `need_redeliver = TRUE` 时：生成第二张取镜单草稿
（`delivery_seq = 2`，`m10_delivery.delivery_status = 'DRAFT'`），订单状态回置 `READY`、
`delivery_status = 'NOTIFIED'`（等顾客来取），**必须重新走 P05 四道校验**；
5) 若为换新（`REPLACE`）则同时生成新的 `WARRANTY_CARD` 行，质保从新交付日起算；
6) 调 M10-09 创建 `followup_type = 'REPAIR'` 的复查计划（可选）；
7) 写状态日志（`action_code = 'REPAIR_COMPLETED'`）。

> **为什么维修后必须重新交付？** 维修后眼镜回到顾客手上，在业务上是一次**新交付**，必须重新核对
> 参数（尤其是换镜片后度数是否与订单一致）。若复用原取镜单，会丢失「维修后是否核对过」的记录，
> 而返修后参数错配恰恰是最高发的投诉来源。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

`created_at DESC` 排序；3) 派生字段：`is_expired`、`remain_days`（相对当前日期计算）；
4) `repair_count` 与订单主表一致（对账校验点）；5) 支持质保卡号 / 手机号模糊查
（`idx_m10_warranty_phone` / `idx_m10_warranty_card`），供前台凭手机号查询。

```
GET /api/v1/m10/orders/warranty/repairs?status=PENDING&page=1&page_size=20
```

**响应**

```json
{

      {

        "order_no": "PO202610310021",

        "repair_status": "PENDING",

      }
    ],

    "page_size": 20,
    "pages": 1
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
```

('PENDING','REPAIRING','OUTSOURCED')`）；2) `status` 支持 `PENDING`（待判定）/ `REPAIRING`
（维修中）/ `REPAIRED`（已修好待交付）；3) `waiting_minutes` 用于超时预警（> 120 分钟标红）；
4) 手机号脱敏；5) 按 `created_at ASC` 排序（先来先处理）。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否。

#### API-33 待催取与超期未取清单

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/orders/pickup-todo?min_ready_days=3&max_remind_times=4&overdue_only=false
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {
        "order_no": "PO202610310020",
        "customer_name": "张先生",
        "customer_phone_masked": "139****5678",
        "ready_at": "2026-11-04T09:15:00+08:00",
        "ready_days": 3,
        "pickup_deadline": "2026-12-04",
        "overdue_days": 0,
        "remind_times": 1,
        "last_remind_at": "2026-11-05T09:00:00+08:00",
        "unpaid_amount_snap": 0.00,
        "total_amount_snap": 3680.00
      },
      {
        "order_no": "PO202610210008",
        "customer_name": "王女士",
        "customer_phone_masked": "138****1234",
        "ready_at": "2026-10-26T09:00:00+08:00",
        "ready_days": 12,
        "pickup_deadline": "2026-11-25",
        "overdue_days": 3,
        "remind_times": 3,
        "last_remind_at": "2026-11-06T09:00:00+08:00",
        "remind_confirmed": false,
        "unpaid_amount_snap": 1610.00,
        "total_amount_snap": 2871.00
      }
    ],
    "total": 15,
    "summary": {
      "pending_pickup": 12,
      "overdue_pickup": 3,
      "remind_limit_reached": 1
    }
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 走部分索引 `idx_m10_order_ready`（`delivery_status IN
('READY_TO_PICK','NOTIFIED')`）；2) `min_ready_days` 过滤到货天数（默认 3，与
`M10.ORDER.PICKUP_REMIND_DAYS` 一致）；3) `overdue_only=true` 走 `idx_m10_order_overdue`；
4) `remind_times` 与 `last_remind_at` 通过 `m10_order_remind` 聚合（子查询 + 部分索引
`idx_m10_order_remind_type`）；5) `remind_confirmed` 来自 `m10_order_hold`；
6) `max_remind_times` 用于筛选「已达催取上限」需转保管的订单；7) 手机号脱敏（催取专员可见全号
走 API-34 预览）。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否。

#### API-34 催取通知

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/remind
Idempotency-Key: 6f5e4d3c-2b1a-0f9e-8d7c-6b5a4f3e2d1c
```

```json
{
  "order_nos": ["PO202610310020", "PO202610280012"],
  "notify_channel": "SMS",
  "template_code": "M10_ORDER_PICKUP_REMIND"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "success_count": 2,
    "failed": [],
    "items": [
      {
        "order_no": "PO202610310020",
        "remind_type": "PICKUP_REMIND",
        "remind_seq": 2,
        "notify_channel": "SMS",
        "notify_target": "139****5678",
        "send_status": "SENT",
        "provider_msg_id": "MSG20261107000123",
        "send_at": "2026-11-07T09:00:05+08:00"
      }
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 逐单校验：订单处于 `READY` 且 `delivery_status IN ('READY_TO_PICK','NOTIFIED')`，
否则跳过并记 `failed`；2) **催取次数上限**：`remind_seq <= M10.ORDER.PICKUP_REMIND_MAX`（默认 4），
达到上限返回 `"1006042"`；3) 计算 `remind_seq = 该订单该类型已有最大序号 + 1`，写入
`m10_order_remind`（`(order_id, remind_type, remind_seq)` 唯一索引防重复）；
4) 从 `m10_order.customer_phone_enc` 解密手机号，调 M03-05 `POST /internal/v1/message/send`
（API-32），载荷含模板编码与参数（`customer_name` / `ready_days` / `pickup_deadline`）；
5) 回写 `send_status` / `provider_msg_id` / `send_at`；6) `notify_target` **脱敏存储**
（`139****5678`），明文不落库；7) 更新订单 `delivery_status = 'NOTIFIED'` 与
`ready_notified_at`（首次）；8) M03-05 失败时 `send_status = 'FAILED'` 并进 Celery 重试队列
（最多 3 次），不全量失败；9) 单次最多 100 单（`"1006047"`）。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-35 超期转保管登记

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/hold
Idempotency-Key: 0a9b8c7d-6e5f-4a3b-2c1d-0e9f8a7b6c5d
```

```json
{
  "order_no": "PO202610210008",
  "hold_location": "A区-3号柜-2层",
  "hold_fee_rule": "PER_DAY",
  "hold_fee_daily": 5.00,
  "free_hold_days": 15,
  "remark": "已催告 3 次顾客未回应"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "hold_no": "BG202611260002",
    "order_no": "PO202610210008",
    "hold_status": "HOLDING",
    "hold_start_at": "2026-11-26T00:00:00+08:00",
    "hold_location": "A区-3号柜-2层",
    "hold_fee_rule": "PER_DAY",
    "hold_fee_daily_snap": 5.00,
    "hold_fee_snap": 0.00,
    "order_delivery_status": "HOLDING",
    "remind_count": 3
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 订单必须已到货且已超 `pickup_deadline`，否则 `"1006039"`；2) 同一订单不能同时有
两条 `HOLDING` 记录（`uk_m10_order_hold_order` 部分唯一索引，冲突 `"1006042"`）；
3) 保管位置必填（否则 `"1006003"`）；4) `PER_DAY` 规则必须 `hold_fee_daily > 0`（否则 `"40001"`）；
5) 生成 `hold_no`（BG），写 `m10_order_hold`（`hold_start_at = pickup_deadline + 1 天 00:00`）；
6) 从 `m10_order_remind` 聚合回填 `remind_count` / `last_remind_at` / `last_remind_channel`；
7) 更新 `m10_order.delivery_status = 'HOLDING'`；8) 写状态日志（`action_code = 'HOLD_ORDER'`，
`action_source = 'USER'`）；9) APScheduler `hold_fee_accrue` 每日 01:00 更新 `hold_days` 与
`hold_fee_snap` 快照（真值由 M16 结算）。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-36 保管遗失登记与责任判定

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/orders/hold/BG202611280001/loss
```

```json
{
  "hold_no": "BG202611280001",
  "loss_reason": "STORE_LOSS",
  "liability_result": "SHARED",
  "liability_basis": "保管期间仅催告 2 次未达规定 3 次，医院承担 70% 责任；顾客长期未取承担 30% 责任",
  "compensation_amount": 1386.00,
  "approval_no": "SP202612010005"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "hold_no": "BG202611280001",
    "hold_status": "LOST",
    "loss_reason": "STORE_LOSS",
    "liability_result": "SHARED",
    "compensation_amount_snap": 1386.00,
    "order_delivery_status": "LOST",
    "loss_approved_at": "2026-12-01T16:00:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 责任判定依据 ≥ 10 字（`"1006034"`）；2) 审批单号必填（`"1006039"`）；
3) 保管单必须为 `HOLDING`，否则 `"1006019"`；4) 写 `loss_reason` / `loss_at` /
`liability_result` / `liability_basis` / `compensation_amount_snap` / 审批字段；
5) 与数据库 `ck_m10_order_hold_lost` 双重保护（遗失必须原因+时间+责任+依据+审批全非空）；
6) 更新 `hold_status = 'LOST'`、`m10_order.delivery_status = 'LOST'`；
7) 赔偿金额 > 0 时调 M16-08 `POST /internal/v1/refund/apply` 生成赔偿退款（M10 不碰钱）；
8) 写状态日志（`action_code = 'LOSS_ORDER'`）；9) 发布 `m10.order.lost` 事件供 M22 质量分析。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

**请求**


**响应**

3) `hold_days` 与 `hold_fee_snap` 为 APScheduler 每日更新后的快照；4) 手机号脱敏；

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否。

`WARRANTY_CARD`（质保卡 WK）/ `AMEND_RECEIPT`（改单凭条）/ `LAB_ORDER`（加工委托单）；
2) `copy` 支持 `CUSTOMER`（顾客联）/ `STUB`（存根联）；3) 质保卡模板要求订单已 `DELIVERED`，
否则 `"1006030"`；4) 取镜单模板要求存在取镜单，否则 `"40400"`；5) 二维码内容
`M10ORDER|<单号>|<HMAC-SHA256 前 8 位>`（密钥在配置中心）；6) 打印后更新
`m10_delivery.warranty_card_printed` 或打印次数，并写审计日志（M02-06）；
7) 二次打印时 `is_reprint = true`，模板渲染「补打」水印；8) 批量打印上限 200 单（`"1006047"`）。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否。

2) 按 `order_no` 查（`uk_m10_order_no`），不存在返回 `"1006001"`；3) 返回**业务档案**：
光度、眼别、工序、交付、质保、维修次数；金额字段一律为 `_snap` 快照，**调用方不得当作财务真值**；
4) 手机号脱敏返回；5) 明细一次带出（`idx_m10_order_item_order`）；6) SLA P95 ≤ 100ms；
7) 内网鉴权（服务间 token + IP 白名单），权限列写「内部服务」。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否（只读）。

#### API-40 由销售单反建业务档案（P-13）

```
POST /internal/v1/m10/orders/from-sales-order
Idempotency-Key: 7d6c5b4a-3e2f-1a0b-9c8d-7e6f5a4b3c2d
```

```json
{
  "sales_order_no": "SO202610310021",
  "sales_order_id": 71001,
  "source_event_id": "evt_7f8e9d0c1b2a3f4e",
  "patient_id": 10021,
  "customer_name": "王女士",
  "customer_phone": "13800001234",
  "optometry_no": "YG20261031005",
  "prescription_no": null,
  "order_type": "FRAME_GLASSES",
  "store_id": 101,
  "seller_id": 5001,
  "total_amount": 2300.00,
  "paid_amount": 690.00,
  "deposit_amount": 690.00,
  "sales_order_status": "PARTIAL",
  "items": [
    {
      "line_no": 1,
      "item_type": "FRAME",
      "product_code": "FR0012",
      "quantity": 1,
      "unit": "副",
      "eye_side": "NA"
    },
    {
      "line_no": 2,
      "item_type": "LENS",
      "product_code": "LN0158",
      "quantity": 1,
      "unit": "片",
      "eye_side": "OD",
      "sphere": -4.25,
      "cylinder": -0.75,
      "axis": 180,
      "pd_total": 64.00,
      "pd_right": 32.50,
      "pd_left": 31.50
    }
  ]
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310021",
    "sales_order_no": "SO202610310021",
    "is_idempotent": false,
    "status": "PAID",
    "item_count": 2,
    "total_amount_snap": 2300.00,
    "create_path": "M16_PUSH",
    "created_at": "2026-10-31T15:22:31+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) **契约 P-13**，调用方 M16-04（收银台直接开单后的反建）；2) **幂等三道防线**：
①`Idempotency-Key` Redis 幂等（`m10:idem:order:from-sales:{key}` TTL 24h）；
②`uk_m10_order_sales` 唯一索引（先查 `sales_order_no`，命中则返回原档案 `is_idempotent: true`）；
③并发插入冲突时捕获 `IntegrityError`（SQLSTATE `23505`）→ 重新查询并返回已存在档案，
**不报错**；3) 校验 `sales_order_no` 格式（`^SO[0-9]{11}$`），不符返回 `"1006003"`；
4) 按 M16 财务态推导初始业务子态：`DRAFT` → `DRAFT`；`PARTIAL` / `PAID` → `PAID`；
`DELIVERED` → `DELIVERED`（幂等补齐）；5) 明细按 M16 明细映射为 `m10_order_item`，
M16 无光度字段时 `sphere` / `cylinder` / `axis` 允许为空（后续由 M10 补录，不影响交付，
但取镜时提示「光度未登记」）；6) 补全默认值：`create_path = 'M16_PUSH'`、
`need_processing` 按明细判定、`expected_ready_date` 按 `processing_days` 计算；
7) 写状态日志（`action_code = 'CREATE_FROM_SALES'`，`action_source = 'API'`）；
8) SLA P95 ≤ 300ms；9) 发布 `m10.order.created` 事件。

> **两条创建路径的统一收口点：** `sales_order_no` 上的唯一索引 `uk_m10_order_sales`
> 是「一个销售单只能对应一个业务档案」的**唯一实现手段**。路径 A（M10 开单后调 API-24）与
> 路径 B（M16 开单后调 P-13 反建）无论谁先到，第二次写入都会撞到唯一冲突，服务层捕获后转为幂等返回。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-41 取镜状态查询（P-14）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/orders/delivery-status/PO202610310021
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310021",
    "delivery_status": "NOTIFIED",
    "delivery_status_text": "已通知",
    "can_pick": true,
    "check_passed": true,
    "blocking_reason": null,
    "blocking_code": null,
    "paid_full": true,
    "unpaid_amount_snap": 0.00,
    "lab_status": "COMPLETED",
    "lab_progress": {
      "lab_job_no": "JG202611040008",
      "completed_steps": 5,
      "total_steps": 5,
      "qc_result": "PASSED"
    },
    "all_arrived": true,
    "pending_lines": [],
    "ready_at": "2026-11-04T09:15:00+08:00",
    "ready_days": 3,
    "pickup_deadline": "2026-12-04",
    "is_overdue": false,
    "hold_status": null,
    "delivered_at": null,
    "delivery_no": null,
    "next_action_text": "可通知顾客到店取镜"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) **契约 P-14**，调用方 M16-04（收银台展示「可取镜」标记）、M03-05（到货通知与
催取消息组装）；2) 按 `order_no` 查（`uk_m10_order_no`）；3) 组装四道校验结果与卡点：
`can_pick = check_paid AND check_lab_done AND check_all_arrived`（第四道需现场人工确认，
不参与 `can_pick` 判定）；4) `blocking_code` 取第一个失败项（`"1006021"`~`"1006023"`）；
5) `next_action_text` 给前台/客服的下一步动作建议，如「请先缴纳尾款 400.00 元」「加工中，
预计 2 天后到货」；6) `lab_progress` 优先读 M10-07 P-16（P95 ≤ 80ms 要求下最多等 50ms），
超时降级用 `lab_status` 快照；7) SLA P95 ≤ 80ms（本子模块 SLA 最严的接口，缓存 30 秒）。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否（只读）。

#### API-42 取镜完成通知（P-15）

**请求**

```
POST /internal/v1/m10/orders/on-delivered
Idempotency-Key: 4c3b2a1f-0e9d-8c7b-6a5f-4e3d2c1b0a9f
```

```json
{
  "order_no": "PO202610310021",
  "delivery_no": "QJ202611040006",
  "delivered_at": "2026-11-04T10:15:33+08:00",
  "deliver_method": "IN_STORE",
  "warranty_card_no": "WK202611040006",
  "warranty_end_date": "2027-11-04",
  "operator_id": 5001
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310021",
    "delivery_no": "QJ202611040006",
    "delivered_at": "2026-11-04T10:15:33+08:00",
    "order_status": "DELIVERED",
    "delivery_status": "DELIVERED",
    "warranty_card_no": "WK202611040006",
    "warranty_end_date": "2027-11-04",
    "followup_plan_no": "FC202611040002",
    "is_idempotent": false,
    "notified_systems": ["M16-04", "M10-09", "M10-08", "M03-03"]
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) **契约 P-15**，调用方 M16-04、M03-03、M19；2) **幂等**：按 `delivery_no` 查
`m10_delivery`，若已 `COMPLETED` 且 `delivered_at` 一致，直接返回 `is_idempotent: true`
（与事件 `m16.optical.delivered` 二选一，重复到达不重复处理）；3) 校验订单存在（`"1006001"`）、
状态机允许（`READY → DELIVERED`，否则 `"1006019"`）；4) 调用方未提供 `warranty_card_no` 时，
M10 内部生成；5) 置订单 `status = 'DELIVERED'`、`delivery_status = 'DELIVERED'`、
`delivered_at`、`warranty_*`；6) 写状态日志（`action_code = 'DELIVER_ORDER'`，
`action_source = 'API'`）；7) 分发给下游（并行，失败进重试队列不阻断）：
①**M16-04** 回写销售单 `DELIVERED`（调 M16-04 API-18 或由 M16 订阅事件）；
②**M10-09** 创建复查计划（`followup_type = 'POST_FITTING'`，默认 3 个月后）；
③**M10-08** 会员消费回写（事件 `m10.order.delivered`）；
④**M03-03 / M19** 会员与经营分析；8) SLA P95 ≤ 100ms（异步分发给下游后立即返回）。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

### 6.4 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/v1/m10/order.py
"""M10-06 配镜订单与取镜交付 —— 对外路由层。"""

from typing import Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Body, Depends, Header, Path, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.audit import audit_log
from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import success_response
from app.core.security import require_permission
from app.schemas.m10.order import (
    AmendDiffSettleIn,
    DeliveryCreateIn,
    DeliverySignIn,
    HoldLossIn,
    HoldRegisterIn,
    OrderAmendIn,
    OrderCreateIn,
    OrderQueryIn,
    OrderUpdateIn,
    RemindSendIn,
    SupplementCreateIn,
    WarrantyJudgeIn,
    WarrantyRepairCompleteIn,
    WarrantyRepairIn,
)
from app.services.m10.delivery_service import DeliveryService
from app.services.m10.order_amend_service import OrderAmendService
from app.services.m10.order_hold_service import OrderHoldService
from app.services.m10.order_service import OrderService
from app.services.m10.order_warranty_service import OrderWarrantyService

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m10/orders", tags=["M10-06 配镜订单与取镜交付"])


# ==================== 主页与统计 ====================

@router.get("/dashboard", summary="订单主页看板数据")
async def get_dashboard(
    store_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, description="门店 ID，按数据权限自动收敛"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回待办卡片、今日概览、7 日趋势、加工超期预警与最近订单。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.get_dashboard(store_id, user))


@router.get("/statistics", summary="订单业务统计报表")
async def get_statistics(
    date_from: str = Query(..., description="统计开始日 YYYY-MM-DD"),
    date_to: str = Query(..., description="统计结束日 YYYY-MM-DD"),
    store_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, description="门店 ID"),
    group_by: str = Query("STATUS", description="分组维度 STATUS/TYPE/STORE/DAY"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:stat")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """订单量、交付率、超期率、改单率、参数不符率与维修率。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.get_statistics(date_from, date_to, store_id, group_by, user)
    )


# ==================== 订单查询 ====================

@router.get("", summary="订单列表")
async def list_orders(
    query: OrderQueryIn = Depends(),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """多条件分页查询，关键字支持单号 / 姓名 / 手机号 / 验光单号。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.list_orders(query, user))


@router.get("/{order_no}", summary="订单详情")
async def get_order_detail(
    order_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32, description="配镜订单号"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """订单主档 + 明细 + 改单与交付摘要。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.get_detail(order_no, user))


@router.get("/{order_no}/timeline", summary="订单状态轨迹时间轴")
async def get_timeline(
    order_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """双轨（业务子态 / M16 财务态）状态迁移轨迹。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.get_timeline(order_no, user))


@router.get("/by-phone/{phone}", summary="按手机号查订单")
async def get_by_phone(
    phone: str = Path(..., min_length=11, max_length=11),
    only_pending: bool = Query(True, description="仅进行中订单"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """前台凭手机号核验取镜资格。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.get_by_phone(phone, only_pending, user))


# ==================== 订单开立 ====================

@router.post("", summary="新建订单")
@audit_log(event_code="M10_ORDER_CREATE", object_type="m10_order")
async def create_order(
    payload: OrderCreateIn,
    submit: bool = Query(False, description="是否保存后立即提交"),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    request: Request = None,
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:create")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """保存草稿或直接提交；提交时会调用 M16-04 API-24 创建销售单。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.create_order(payload, submit, user,
                               idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
                               trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    )


@router.put("/{order_no}", summary="修改草稿订单")
@audit_log(event_code="M10_ORDER_UPDATE", object_type="m10_order")
async def update_order(
    payload: OrderUpdateIn,
    order_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    request: Request = None,
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:create")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """仅 DRAFT 状态可改，乐观锁 version 校验。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.update_order(order_no, payload, user,
                               trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    )


@router.post("/preview", summary="开单试算")
async def preview_order(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:create")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """调 M01-08 取价 + M16-06 优惠试算，本模块不做金额计算。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.preview_order(payload, user))


@router.post("/{order_no}/submit", summary="提交订单")
@audit_log(event_code="M10_ORDER_SUBMIT", object_type="m10_order")
async def submit_order(
    order_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    request: Request = None,
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:create")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """调用 M16-04 API-24 建销售单，回写 sales_order_no（唯一索引保证幂等）。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.submit_order(order_no, user, idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
                               trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    )


@router.post("/spec-check", summary="光度可做性批量校验")
async def spec_check(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:create")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """代理 M01-08 API-21 批量光度校验，结果缓存 30 分钟。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.spec_check(payload))


@router.post("/labor-urge", summary="催加工")
@audit_log(event_code="M10_ORDER_LABOR_URGE", object_type="m10_order")
async def labor_urge(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:create")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """向 M10-07 发出催工，同一订单 2 小时内仅推送一次。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.labor_urge(payload, user))


# ==================== 状态流转 ====================

@router.post("/{order_no}/cancel", summary="取消订单")
@audit_log(event_code="M10_ORDER_CANCEL", object_type="m10_order")
async def cancel_order(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    order_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    request: Request = None,
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:create")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """仅未收款订单可直接取消；已收款需先由 M16 退费。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.cancel_order(order_no, payload, user,
                               trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    )


@router.post("/{order_no}/dispatch-lab", summary="手动派工")
@audit_log(event_code="M10_ORDER_DISPATCH", object_type="m10_order")
async def dispatch_lab(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    order_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    request: Request = None,
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:create")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """调 M10-07 P-17 派工，回写 lab_job_no 并置 IN_PRODUCTION。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.dispatch_lab(order_no, payload, user,
                               idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
                               trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    )


@router.post("/{order_no}/mark-ready", summary="无需加工订单标记到货")
@audit_log(event_code="M10_ORDER_MARK_READY", object_type="m10_order")
async def mark_ready(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    order_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:create")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """纯现货配件订单直接置 READY 并计算取镜截止日。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.mark_ready(order_no, payload, user))


@router.post("/{order_no}/sync-sales", summary="主动同步 M16 财务态")
async def sync_sales(
    order_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:update")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """事件丢失时的人工补偿，也供每日对账任务调用。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.sync_sales_status(order_no, user))


# ==================== 改单与补单 ====================

@router.post("/{order_no}/amend/preview", summary="改单试算")
async def preview_amend(
    payload: OrderAmendIn,
    order_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),

):

    svc = OrderAmendService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.preview_amend(order_no, payload, user))


@router.post("/{order_no}/amend", summary="提交改单")
@audit_log(event_code="M10_ORDER_AMEND", object_type="m10_order")
async def submit_amend(
    payload: OrderAmendIn,
    order_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    request: Request = None,
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:order:amend")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """加工中改单需质检退货单号；差额由 M16 补收或退差。"""
    svc = OrderAmendService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.submit_amend(order_no, payload, user,
                               idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
                               trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    )


@router.get("/{order_no}/amends", summary="改单记录列表")
async def list_amends(
    order_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),

):

    return success_response(await svc.list_amends(order_no, user))


@router.post("/amend/{amend_no}/approve", summary="改单审批结果回调")
```

```python
# app/api/internal/m10/order.py
"""M10-06 配镜订单与取镜交付 —— 内部契约路由（P-12 / P-13 / P-14 / P-15）。"""

from typing import Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header, Path, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import success_response
from app.core.security import require_internal_service
from app.schemas.m10.order import FromSalesOrderIn, OnDeliveredIn
from app.services.m10.delivery_service import DeliveryService
from app.services.m10.order_service import OrderService

internal_router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m10/orders",
                            tags=["M10-06 内部契约"])


@internal_router.get("/{order_no}", summary="P-12 取配镜订单业务档案")
async def p12_get_order(
    order_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32, description="配镜订单号 PO..."),
    service_name: str = "unknown",
    caller=Depends(require_internal_service),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """供 M16-04 / M22 / M17 读取业务档案；金额字段为快照，非财务真值。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.internal_get_order(order_no, caller=caller,
                                     service_name=service_name)
    )


@internal_router.post("/from-sales-order", summary="P-13 由销售单反建业务档案")
async def p13_from_sales_order(
    payload: FromSalesOrderIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    request: Request = None,
    caller=Depends(require_internal_service),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """三条幂等防线：Idempotency-Key、uk_m10_order_sales 唯一索引、23505 冲突转幂等返回。"""
    svc = OrderService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.create_from_sales_order(payload, caller=caller,
                                          idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
                                          trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    )


@internal_router.get("/delivery-status/{order_no}", summary="P-14 取镜状态查询")
async def p14_delivery_status(
    order_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    caller=Depends(require_internal_service),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """四道校验结论 + 卡点错误码 + 下一步动作建议，P95 ≤ 80ms。"""
    svc = DeliveryService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.get_delivery_status(order_no, caller=caller))


@internal_router.post("/on-delivered", summary="P-15 取镜完成通知")
async def p15_on_delivered(
    payload: OnDeliveredIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    request: Request = None,
    caller=Depends(require_internal_service),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """幂等（按 delivery_no）；异步分发 M16-04 / M10-09 / M10-08 / M03-03。"""
    svc = DeliveryService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.on_delivered(payload, caller=caller,
                               idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
                               trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    )
```
