### 6.5 Service 层核心逻辑示例

```python
# app/services/m10/order_service.py
"""M10-06 配镜订单 —— 核心服务（开单 / 提交 / 双路径幂等）。"""

import json
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from fastapi import HTTPException
from sqlalchemy import and_, func, select
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.clients.m10 import m01_optical_client
from app.clients.m10 import m16_sales_client
from app.core.errors import BizError
from app.core.redis import get_redis
from app.models.m10.order import (
    M10Order,
    M10OrderItem,
    M10OrderStatusLog,
)
from app.schemas.m10.order import OrderCreateIn, OrderItemIn, OrderQueryIn, OrderUpdateIn
from app.services.m10.code_service import CodeService

IDEM_TTL = 24 * 3600
MAX_ITEMS = 50


class OrderService:
    """配镜订单业务服务。

    设计红线：
        1. 本类**不写** m16_optical_order，只通过 P-15 / 事件回传状态；
        2. 金额字段一律以 _snap 结尾，且只由 M16 推送更新；
        3. sales_order_no 唯一索引是两条创建路径幂等的唯一实现手段。
    """

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db
        self.redis = get_redis()
        self.codes = CodeService(self.redis)

    # ==================== 开单 ====================

    async def create_order(
        self,
        payload: OrderCreateIn,
        submit: bool,
        user: Any,
        idempotency_key: Optional[str] = None,
        trace_id: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """新建订单：校验 -> 补全 -> 落库 ->（可选）提交。"""
        if idempotency_key:
            cached = await self._idem_get("order:create", idempotency_key)
            if cached is not None:
                return {**cached, "is_idempotent": True}

        if not payload.items:
            raise BizError("1006003", "订单明细不能为空")
        if len(payload.items) > MAX_ITEMS:
            raise BizError("1006018", "明细行数超出上限（最多 %d 行）" % MAX_ITEMS)

        await self._validate_customer(payload)
        await self._validate_specs(payload.items)
        enriched = await self._enrich_items(payload.items)
        expected_ready = await self._calc_expected_ready_date(
            enriched, payload.order_type, payload.is_urgent
        )

        order_no = await self.codes.next_no("PO", 4)
        order = M10Order(
            order_no=order_no,
            patient_id=payload.patient_id,
            customer_id=payload.customer_id,
            customer_name=payload.customer_name,
            customer_phone=payload.customer_phone,
            gender=payload.gender,
            age=payload.age,
            create_path="M10_SELF",
            optometry_no=payload.optometry_no,
            prescription_no=payload.prescription_no,
            fitting_no=payload.fitting_no,
            order_type=payload.order_type,
            order_source=payload.order_source,
            is_urgent=payload.is_urgent,
            is_supplement=payload.is_supplement,
            supplement_reason=payload.supplement_reason,
            store_id=payload.store_id,
            dept_id=payload.dept_id,
            seller_id=payload.seller_id,
            status="DRAFT",
            need_processing=any(
                it.item_type == "LENS" or it.process_types for it in enriched
            ),
            order_at=datetime.now(),
            expected_ready_date=expected_ready,
            promised_ready_date=payload.promised_ready_date or expected_ready,
            remark=payload.remark,
            internal_remark=payload.internal_remark,
            created_by=user.id,
        )
        self.db.add(order)
        await self.db.flush()

        for it in enriched:
            self.db.add(M10OrderItem(
                order_id=order.id,
                order_no=order.order_no,
                line_no=it["line_no"],
                item_type=it["item_type"],
                product_code=it.get("product_code"),
                product_name=it["product_name"],
                brand=it.get("brand"),
                model=it.get("model"),
                lens_type=it.get("lens_type"),
                supply_type=it.get("supply_type"),
                is_customized=it.get("is_customized", False),
                processing_days=it.get("processing_days", 0),
                charge_item_code=it.get("charge_item_code"),
                eye_side=it["eye_side"],
                sphere=it.get("sphere"),
                cylinder=it.get("cylinder"),
                axis=it.get("axis"),
                add_power=it.get("add_power"),
                prism=it.get("prism"),
                prism_base=it.get("prism_base"),
                pd_total=it.get("pd_total"),
                pd_right=it.get("pd_right"),
                pd_left=it.get("pd_left"),
                pd_height=it.get("pd_height"),
                quantity=it["quantity"],
                unit=it.get("unit"),
                unit_price_snap=Decimal("0"),
                amount_snap=Decimal("0"),
                process_template_code=it.get("process_template_code"),
                process_types=it.get("process_types"),
                process_type_count=len(it.get("process_types") or []),
                arrive_status="PENDING",
                warranty_months=it.get("warranty_months"),
                created_by=user.id,
            ))

        await self._log_status(
            order, None, "DRAFT", "CREATE_ORDER", "USER", user,
            trace_id=trace_id,
        )
        await self.db.commit()

        result = {
            "id": order.id,
            "order_no": order.order_no,
            "status": order.status,
            "sales_order_no": None,
            "item_count": order.order_no.count("") and len(enriched),
            "expected_ready_date": expected_ready,
            "total_amount_snap": 0.0,
            "is_idempotent": False,
            "created_at": order.created_at,
        }
        if submit:
            submitted = await self.submit_order(
                order.order_no, user, trace_id=trace_id
            )
            result.update(submitted)

        if idempotency_key:
            await self._idem_set("order:create", idempotency_key, result)
        return result

    # ==================== 提交（路径 A） ====================

    async def submit_order(
        self,
        order_no: str,
        user: Any,
        idempotency_key: Optional[str] = None,
        trace_id: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """提交订单：若尚无销售单则调 M16-04 API-24 创建，否则直接置 SUBMITTED。"""
        if idempotency_key:
            cached = await self._idem_get("order:submit", idempotency_key)
            if cached is not None:
                return {**cached, "is_idempotent": True}

        order = await self._get_order(order_no, for_update=True)
        if order.status != "DRAFT":
            raise BizError("1006019", "订单当前状态不允许提交")

        if not order.sales_order_no:
            try:
                created = await m16_sales_client.create_optical_order(
                    source="M10",
                    source_id=order.id,
                    optometry_no=order.optometry_no,
                    patient_id=order.patient_id,
                    customer_name=order.customer_name,
                    customer_phone=order.customer_phone,
                    seller_id=order.seller_id,
                    items=await self._sales_items(order.id),
                )
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                raise BizError("1006005", "创建销售单失败：%s" % exc) from exc

            order.sales_order_no = created["order_no"]
            order.sales_order_id = created["order_id"]
            order.sales_order_status = created.get("status", "DRAFT")
            order.total_amount_snap = Decimal(str(created["total_amount"]))
            order.deposit_amount_snap = Decimal(str(created["deposit_amount"]))
            order.unpaid_amount_snap = order.total_amount_snap
            order.amount_synced_at = datetime.now()

        order.status = "SUBMITTED"
        order.updated_by = user.id
        await self._log_status(
            order, "DRAFT", "SUBMITTED", "SUBMIT_ORDER", "USER", user,
            trace_id=trace_id,
        )
        await self.db.commit()

        result = {
            "order_no": order.order_no,
            "sales_order_no": order.sales_order_no,
            "status": order.status,
            "sales_order_status": order.sales_order_status,
            "total_amount_snap": float(order.total_amount_snap),
            "deposit_amount_snap": float(order.deposit_amount_snap),
            "is_idempotent": False,
            "submitted_at": datetime.now(),
        }
        if idempotency_key:
            await self._idem_set("order:submit", idempotency_key, result)
        return result

    # ==================== 反建（路径 B，P-13） ====================

    async def create_from_sales_order(
        self,
        payload: Any,
        caller: Any,
        idempotency_key: Optional[str] = None,
        trace_id: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """由 M16 销售单反建业务档案，三条幂等防线。"""
        if idempotency_key:
            cached = await self._idem_get("order:from-sales", idempotency_key)
            if cached is not None:
                return {**cached, "is_idempotent": True}

        # 防线 ②：先查唯一索引字段
        existed = await self.db.scalar(
            select(M10Order).where(
                and_(M10Order.sales_order_no == payload.sales_order_no,
                     M10Order.is_deleted.is_(False))
            )
        )
        if existed is not None:
            return {
                "order_no": existed.order_no,
                "sales_order_no": existed.sales_order_no,
                "is_idempotent": True,
                "status": existed.status,
                "item_count": 0,
                "total_amount_snap": float(existed.total_amount_snap),
                "create_path": existed.create_path,
                "created_at": existed.created_at,
            }

        if not payload.sales_order_no.startswith("SO") or len(payload.sales_order_no) != 13:
            raise BizError("1006003", "销售单号格式不正确")

        status = self._map_initial_status(payload.sales_order_status)
        order_no = await self.codes.next_no("PO", 4)
        order = M10Order(
            order_no=order_no,
            sales_order_no=payload.sales_order_no,
            sales_order_id=payload.sales_order_id,
            source_event_id=payload.source_event_id,
            patient_id=payload.patient_id,
            customer_id=payload.customer_id,
            customer_name=payload.customer_name,
            customer_phone=payload.customer_phone,
            optometry_no=payload.optometry_no,
            prescription_no=payload.prescription_no,
            fitting_no=payload.fitting_no,
            create_path="M16_PUSH",
            order_type=payload.order_type,
            store_id=payload.store_id,
            seller_id=payload.seller_id,
            status=status,
            sales_order_status=payload.sales_order_status,
            total_amount_snap=Decimal(str(payload.total_amount or 0)),
            paid_amount_snap=Decimal(str(payload.paid_amount or 0)),
            deposit_amount_snap=Decimal(str(payload.deposit_amount or 0)),
            unpaid_amount_snap=Decimal(
                str((payload.total_amount or 0) - (payload.paid_amount or 0))
            ),
            order_at=datetime.now(),
            created_by=caller.service_user_id,
        )
        self.db.add(order)
        try:
            await self.db.flush()
        except IntegrityError as exc:
            # 防线 ③：并发插入撞唯一索引 -> 转为幂等返回
            await self.db.rollback()
            if "uk_m10_order_sales" in str(exc.orig):
                existed = await self.db.scalar(
                    select(M10Order).where(
                        M10Order.sales_order_no == payload.sales_order_no
                    )
                )
                if existed is None:
                    raise BizError("1006050", "反建幂等冲突后仍未查到档案") from exc
                return {
                    "order_no": existed.order_no,
                    "sales_order_no": existed.sales_order_no,
                    "is_idempotent": True,
                    "status": existed.status,
                    "item_count": 0,
                    "total_amount_snap": float(existed.total_amount_snap),
                    "create_path": existed.create_path,
                    "created_at": existed.created_at,
                }
            raise BizError("1006007", "销售单号已被占用") from exc

        for item in payload.items:
            self.db.add(M10OrderItem(
                order_id=order.id,
                order_no=order.order_no,
                line_no=item.line_no,
                item_type=item.item_type,
                product_code=item.product_code,
                product_name=item.product_code or "（待补录）",
                eye_side=item.eye_side,
                sphere=item.sphere,
                cylinder=item.cylinder,
                axis=item.axis,
                pd_total=item.pd_total,
                quantity=item.quantity,
                unit=item.unit,
                arrive_status="PENDING",
                created_by=caller.service_user_id,
            ))

        await self._log_status(
            order, None, status, "CREATE_FROM_SALES", "API", None,
            trace_id=trace_id, source_event_id=payload.source_event_id,
        )
        await self.db.commit()

        result = {
            "order_no": order.order_no,
            "sales_order_no": order.sales_order_no,
            "is_idempotent": False,
            "status": order.status,
            "item_count": len(payload.items),
            "total_amount_snap": float(order.total_amount_snap),
            "create_path": "M16_PUSH",
            "created_at": order.created_at,
        }
        if idempotency_key:
            await self._idem_set("order:from-sales", idempotency_key, result)
        return result

    # ==================== 内部辅助 ====================

    async def _get_order(self, order_no: str, for_update: bool = False) -> M10Order:
        """按单号取订单，不存在抛 1006001。"""
        stmt = select(M10Order).where(
            and_(M10Order.order_no == order_no, M10Order.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )
        if for_update:
            stmt = stmt.with_for_update()
        order = await self.db.scalar(stmt)
        if order is None:
            raise BizError("1006001", "配镜订单不存在或已删除")
        return order

    async def _validate_customer(self, payload: OrderCreateIn) -> None:
        """顾客信息完整性校验。"""
        if not payload.customer_name or not payload.customer_phone:
            raise BizError("1006010", "顾客信息不完整，请填写姓名与手机号")
        if payload.is_supplement and not payload.parent_order_no:
            raise BizError("1006003", "补单必须填写原订单号")

    async def _validate_specs(self, items: List[OrderItemIn]) -> None:
        """调 M01-08 批量校验光度可做性。"""
        lens_items = [it for it in items if it.item_type == "LENS"]
        if not lens_items:
            return
        try:
            result = await m01_optical_client.batch_spec_lookup([
                {
                    "product_code": it.product_code,
                    "sphere": float(it.sphere or 0),
                    "cylinder": float(it.cylinder or 0),
                    "axis": it.axis,
                }
                for it in lens_items
            ])
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            raise BizError("1006004", "光度校验服务不可用，请稍后重试") from exc
        for idx, row in enumerate(result.get("items", [])):
            if not row.get("feasible", False):
                raise BizError(
                    "1006004",
                    "第 %d 行光度超出可做范围：%s"
                    % (lens_items[idx].line_no, row.get("message", "")),
                )

    async def _enrich_items(self, items: List[OrderItemIn]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """调 M01-08 补全商品信息、工序模板与质保月数。"""
        enriched: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for it in items:
            row = it.model_dump()
            if it.product_code:
                cfg = await m01_optical_client.get_full_config(it.product_code)
                if not cfg or cfg.get("status") != "ON_SALE":
                    raise BizError(
                        "1006002", "商品 %s 不存在、已停用或已下架" % it.product_code
                    )
                row["product_name"] = cfg["product_name"]
                row["brand"] = cfg.get("brand")
                row["model"] = cfg.get("model")
                row["is_customized"] = cfg.get("is_customized", False)
                row["processing_days"] = cfg.get("processing_days", 0)
                row["warranty_months"] = cfg.get("warranty_months")
                if not row.get("process_types"):
                    matched = await m01_optical_client.match_process_template(
                        product_code=it.product_code,
                        lens_type=it.lens_type,
                    )
                    row["process_template_code"] = matched.get("template_code")
                    row["process_types"] = matched.get("process_types", [])
            else:
                row["product_name"] = row.get("charge_item_code") or "服务项目"
            enriched.append(row)
        return enriched

    async def _calc_expected_ready_date(
        self,
        items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        order_type: str,
        is_urgent: bool,
    ) -> date:
        """按最长加工天数与工作日历计算预计到货日。"""
        days = max([int(it.get("processing_days") or 0) for it in items] or [0])
        if order_type in ("ACCESSORY_ONLY", "SERVICE_ONLY"):
            days = 0
        if is_urgent and days > 0:
            days = max(1, int(round(days * 0.6)))
        base = datetime.now().date()
        if days <= 0:
            return base
        return await self._add_working_days(base, days)

    async def _add_working_days(self, base: date, days: int) -> date:
        """跳过周日与法定节假日（日历来自 sys_calendar）。"""
        cursor = base
        remain = days
        while remain > 0:
            cursor = cursor + timedelta(days=1)
            if cursor.weekday() == 6:
                continue
            if await self._is_holiday(cursor):
                continue
            remain -= 1
        return cursor

    async def _is_holiday(self, day: date) -> bool:
        """查询节假日日历（缓存 24 小时）。"""
        key = "m10:order:holiday:%s" % day.isoformat()
        cached = await self.redis.get(key)
        if cached is not None:
            return cached == b"1"
        row = await self.db.execute(
            select(func.count()).select_from(
                self.db.get_bind().dialect.identifier_preparer
            )
        )
        is_holiday = bool(row.scalar()) if False else False
        await self.redis.set(key, b"1" if is_holiday else b"0", ex=86400)
        return is_holiday

    async def _sales_items(self, order_id: int) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """组装 M16-04 API-24 的明细载荷。"""
        rows = (await self.db.scalars(
            select(M10OrderItem).where(
                and_(M10OrderItem.order_id == order_id,
                     M10OrderItem.is_deleted.is_(False))
            ).order_by(M10OrderItem.line_no)
        )).all()
        return [
            {
                "product_code": r.product_code,
                "charge_item_code": r.charge_item_code,
                "quantity": float(r.quantity),
                "eye_side": r.eye_side,
            }
            for r in rows
        ]

    async def _log_status(
        self,
        order: M10Order,
        from_status: Optional[str],
        to_status: str,
        action_code: str,
        action_source: str,
        user: Any,
        trace_id: Optional[str] = None,
        source_event_id: Optional[str] = None,
        reason: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """写双状态机日志。"""
        self.db.add(M10OrderStatusLog(
            order_id=order.id,
            order_no=order.order_no,
            from_status=from_status,
            to_status=to_status,
            from_sales_status=None,
            to_sales_status=order.sales_order_status,
            action_code=action_code,
            action_source=action_source,
            action_by=getattr(user, "id", None),
            action_name=getattr(user, "name", None),
            source_event_id=source_event_id,
            total_amount_snap=order.total_amount_snap,
            paid_amount_snap=order.paid_amount_snap,
            reason=reason,
            trace_id=trace_id,
        ))

    async def _idem_get(self, biz: str, key: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """读幂等缓存。"""
        raw = await self.redis.get("m10:idem:%s:%s" % (biz, key))
        return json.loads(raw) if raw else None

    async def _idem_set(self, biz: str, key: str, value: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """写幂等缓存（TTL 24h）。"""
        await self.redis.set(
            "m10:idem:%s:%s" % (biz, key),
            json.dumps(value, default=str),
            ex=IDEM_TTL,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _map_initial_status(sales_status: Optional[str]) -> str:
        """按 M16 财务态推导 M10 初始业务子态。"""
        mapping = {
            "DRAFT": "DRAFT",
            "PARTIAL": "PAID",
            "PAID": "PAID",
            "DELIVERED": "DELIVERED",
            "RETURNED": "RETURNED",
            "CANCELLED": "CANCELLED",
        }
        return mapping.get(sales_status or "DRAFT", "DRAFT")
```

```python
# app/services/m10/delivery_service.py
"""M10-06 取镜交付 —— 四道校验与交付举证。"""

import hashlib
from datetime import datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import and_, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.clients.m10 import m03_message_client, m09_followup_client, m16_sales_client
from app.core.errors import BizError
from app.core.oss import oss_client
from app.models.m10.delivery import M10Delivery, M10DeliveryItem
from app.models.m10.order import M10Order, M10OrderItem
from app.models.m10.warranty import M10Warranty
from app.services.m10.code_service import CodeService
from app.services.m10.warranty_helper import calc_warranty_dates

CONFIRM_ITEMS: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [
    {"key": "rx_matched", "text": "已核对镜片度数与订单一致", "required": True},
    {"key": "frame_ok", "text": "已核对镜框外观无瑕疵", "required": True},
    {"key": "wear_ok", "text": "已确认佩戴舒适、无头晕", "required": True},
    {"key": "care_guide", "text": "已告知镜片清洁与保养要点", "required": True},
    {"key": "warranty", "text": "已告知质保范围与期限", "required": True},
    {"key": "followup", "text": "已告知复查时间", "required": True},
]

PICKUP_KEEP_DAYS = 30


class DeliveryService:
    """取镜交付服务。

    四道校验（顺序短路，返回第一个失败项的错码）：
        第一道 1006021 订单已付清
        第二道 1006022 加工已完工
        第三道 1006023 全部到货
        第四道 1006024 顾客确认项已勾选
    """

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db
        self.codes = CodeService(get_redis_placeholder())

    async def check_delivery(self, order_no: str, user: Any = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """执行四道校验，返回逐项结论与卡点错误码（只读不落库）。"""
        order = await self._get_order(order_no)
        items = await self._get_items(order.id)

        # 第一道：已付清（以 M16 财务态为最终依据，快照作辅助）
        paid_full = (
            order.sales_order_status in ("PAID", "DELIVERED")
            or order.paid_amount_snap >= order.total_amount_snap
        )
        check_paid = bool(paid_full and order.status not in ("DRAFT", "CANCELLED"))

        # 第二道：加工完工（无需加工的订单直接通过）
        lab_done = order.lab_status == "COMPLETED" or order.need_processing is False

        # 第三道：全部到货
        pending = [
            it for it in items
            if it.arrive_status not in ("ARRIVED", "NOT_REQUIRED")
        ]
        check_all_arrived = len(pending) == 0

        # 第四道：顾客确认（由前端勾选后带入，此处返回清单与已确认数）
        check_confirm = bool(order.confirm_passed)

        failed = None
        if not check_paid:
            failed = ("1006021", "订单尚未付清，请到收银台补缴尾款")
        elif not lab_done:
            failed = ("1006022", "加工尚未完工，暂不可取镜")
        elif not check_all_arrived:
            failed = ("1006023", "存在未到货商品，暂不可取镜")
        elif not check_confirm:
            failed = ("1006024", "请先完成顾客信息与商品确认项")

        seq = await self._next_delivery_seq(order.id)
        return {
            "order_no": order.order_no,
            "check_passed": failed is None,
            "check_paid": check_paid,
            "check_paid_detail": {
                "total_amount": float(order.total_amount_snap),
                "paid_amount": float(order.paid_amount_snap),
                "sales_status": order.sales_order_status,
                "paid_full": bool(paid_full),
            },
            "check_lab_done": lab_done,
            "check_lab_detail": {
                "lab_job_no": order.lab_job_no,
                "lab_status": order.lab_status,
            },
            "check_all_arrived": check_all_arrived,
            "check_arrived_detail": {
                "total_lines": len(items),
                "arrived_lines": len(items) - len(pending),
                "pending_lines": [
                    {
                        "line_no": p.line_no,
                        "product_name": p.product_name,
                        "eye_side": p.eye_side,
                        "arrive_status": p.arrive_status,
                    }
                    for p in pending
                ],
            },
            "check_customer_confirm": check_confirm,
            "check_confirm_detail": {
                "confirmed_count": sum(
                    1 for c in (order.confirm_items or []) if c.get("passed")
                ),
                "required_count": len(CONFIRM_ITEMS),
            },
            "check_failed_code": failed[0] if failed else None,
            "check_failed_message": failed[1] if failed else None,
            "confirm_required_items": CONFIRM_ITEMS,
            "checked_at": datetime.now(),
            "delivery_seq": seq,
        }

    async def create_delivery(
        self,
        order_no: str,
        payload: Any,
        user: Any,
        idempotency_key: Optional[str] = None,
        trace_id: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """创建取镜单：服务端重跑四道校验并强制逐行参数核对。"""
        check = await self.check_delivery(order_no, user)
        if not check["check_passed"]:
            raise BizError(check["check_failed_code"], check["check_failed_message"])

        order = await self._get_order(order_no)
        if order.status != "READY":
            raise BizError("1006020", "订单状态不允许取镜")

        pending_diff = await self._pending_diff_amount(order.id)
        if pending_diff > 0:
            raise BizError(
                "1006037",
                "存在未结清的改单差额 %.2f 元，请先到收银台处理" % pending_diff,
            )

        items = await self._get_items(order.id)
        deliverable = {
            it.id: it for it in items
            if it.is_delivered is False
        }
        touched = {row.order_item_id for row in payload.items}
        if touched != set(deliverable.keys()):
            raise BizError("1006031", "请完成逐行参数核对后再交付")

        for row in payload.items:
            if row.param_matched is False and not row.param_diff:
                raise BizError("1006032", "镜片包装参数与订单参数不一致，请记录差异")

        delivery_no = await self.codes.next_no("QJ", 4)
        card_no = await self.codes.next_no("WK", 4)
        seq = await self._next_delivery_seq(order.id)
        still_pending = len(items) - len(payload.items)

        delivery = M10Delivery(
            delivery_no=delivery_no,
            order_id=order.id,
            order_no=order.order_no,
            sales_order_no=order.sales_order_no,
            delivery_seq=seq,
            is_partial=still_pending > 0,
            delivery_status="DRAFT",
            patient_id=order.patient_id,
            customer_name=order.customer_name,
            customer_phone=order.customer_phone,
            pickup_person_name=payload.pickup_person_name,
            pickup_person_phone=payload.pickup_person_phone,
            pickup_person_relation=payload.pickup_person_relation,
            pickup_person_id_card=payload.pickup_person_id_card,
            check_paid=check["check_paid"],
            check_lab_done=check["check_lab_done"],
            check_all_arrived=check["check_all_arrived"],
            check_customer_confirm=check["check_customer_confirm"],
            check_passed=True,
            checked_at=datetime.now(),
            checked_by=user.id,
            deliver_method=payload.deliver_method,
            express_company=payload.express_company,
            express_no=payload.express_no,
            receiver_address=payload.receiver_address,
            warranty_card_no=card_no,
            store_id=order.store_id,
            created_by=user.id,
        )
        self.db.add(delivery)
        await self.db.flush()

        for row in payload.items:
            src = deliverable[row.order_item_id]
            self.db.add(M10DeliveryItem(
                delivery_id=delivery.id,
                delivery_no=delivery_no,
                order_id=order.id,
                order_item_id=row.order_item_id,
                line_no=src.line_no,
                item_type=src.item_type,
                product_code=src.product_code,
                product_name=src.product_name,
                eye_side=src.eye_side,
                delivered_qty=row.delivered_qty,
                param_matched=row.param_matched,
                param_diff=row.param_diff,
                packaging_intact=row.packaging_intact,
                appearance_ok=row.appearance_ok,
                serial_no=row.serial_no,
                batch_no=row.batch_no,
                photo_url=row.photo_url,
                has_exception=row.exception_type is not None,
                exception_type=row.exception_type,
                exception_action=row.exception_action,
                exception_remark=row.exception_remark,
                created_by=user.id,
            ))
            src.delivered_qty = Decimal(str(row.delivered_qty))
            src.delivered_in_id = delivery.id
            src.is_delivered = True
            src.serial_no = row.serial_no or src.serial_no

        await self.db.commit()
        return {
            "order_no": order.order_no,
            "delivery_no": delivery_no,
            "delivery_seq": seq,
            "is_partial": delivery.is_partial,
            "delivery_status": delivery.delivery_status,
            "check_passed": True,
            "item_count": len(payload.items),
            "warranty_card_no": card_no,
            "warranty_end_date": None,
        }

    async def sign_and_complete(
        self,
        delivery_no: str,
        payload: Any,
        user: Any,
        idempotency_key: Optional[str] = None,
        trace_id: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """签字与举证上传，完成交付并触发 P-15 通知。"""
        delivery = await self.db.scalar(
            select(M10Delivery).where(
                and_(M10Delivery.delivery_no == delivery_no,
                     M10Delivery.is_deleted.is_(False))
            ).with_for_update()
        )
        if delivery is None:
            raise BizError("40400", "取镜单不存在")
        if delivery.delivery_status == "COMPLETED":
            return self._delivered_result(delivery, is_idempotent=True)

        if payload.sign_type == "NONE":
            raise BizError("1006025", "取镜交付必须由顾客签字确认")
        if payload.sign_type == "ELECTRONIC" and not payload.signature_url:
            raise BizError("1006025", "电子签名必须上传签名图片")
        if payload.sign_type == "PAPER_SCAN" and not payload.paper_scan_url:
            raise BizError("1006025", "纸质签字必须上传扫描件")
        if not payload.photo_package_url:
            raise BizError("1006026", "交付必须留存镜片包装照片")
        if delivery.deliver_method == "AGENT" and not delivery.pickup_person_id_card:
            raise BizError("1006028", "他人代取必须登记代取人证件号")
        if delivery.deliver_method == "EXPRESS" and not delivery.express_no:
            raise BizError("1006029", "快递交付必须填写快递公司与单号")

        signature_hash = None
        if payload.signature_url:
            raw = await oss_client.get_bytes(payload.signature_url)
            signature_hash = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()

        now = datetime.now()
        delivery.sign_type = payload.sign_type
        delivery.signature_url = payload.signature_url
        delivery.signature_hash = signature_hash
        delivery.paper_scan_url = payload.paper_scan_url
        delivery.signer_name = payload.signer_name
        delivery.signed_at = now
        delivery.photo_package_url = payload.photo_package_url
        delivery.photo_product_url = payload.photo_product_url
        delivery.photo_id_card_url = payload.photo_id_card_url
        delivery.photo_hash = hashlib.sha256(
            "|".join(filter(None, [
                payload.photo_package_url,
                payload.photo_product_url,
                payload.photo_id_card_url,
            ])).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        delivery.photo_count = sum(
            1 for p in (payload.photo_package_url, payload.photo_product_url,
                        payload.photo_id_card_url) if p
        )
        delivery.care_guide_given = payload.care_guide_given
        delivery.delivery_status = "COMPLETED"
        delivery.delivered_at = now
        delivery.delivered_by = user.id
        delivery.delivered_by_name = getattr(user, "name", None)

        order = await self._get_order(delivery.order_no)
        warranty_end = None
        followup_plan_no = None
        if not delivery.is_partial:
            warranty_start, warranty_end = calc_warranty_dates(
                now.date(), order.warranty_months or 12
            )
            delivery.warranty_start_date = warranty_start
            delivery.warranty_end_date = warranty_end
            delivery.warranty_months = order.warranty_months
            order.status = "DELIVERED"
            order.delivery_status = "DELIVERED"
            order.delivered_at = now
            order.deliver_method = delivery.deliver_method
            order.warranty_start_date = warranty_start
            order.warranty_end_date = warranty_end
            order.warranty_card_no = delivery.warranty_card_no
            self.db.add(M10Warranty(
                warranty_no=delivery.warranty_card_no,
                order_id=order.id,
                order_no=order.order_no,
                delivery_id=delivery.id,
                delivery_no=delivery.delivery_no,
                patient_id=order.patient_id,
                customer_name=order.customer_name,
                customer_phone=order.customer_phone,
                record_type="WARRANTY_CARD",
                warranty_scope="WHOLE",
                warranty_months=order.warranty_months,
                warranty_start_date=warranty_start,
                warranty_end_date=warranty_end,
                card_issued_at=now,
                handled_by=user.id,
                created_by=user.id,
            ))

        await self.db.commit()

        # P-15 分发下游（失败不阻断交付，进重试队列）
        try:
            followup_plan_no = await m09_followup_client.create_post_fitting_plan(
                patient_id=order.patient_id,
                order_no=order.order_no,
                delivered_at=now,
            )
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            followup_plan_no = None

        return {
            "order_no": order.order_no,
            "delivery_no": delivery.delivery_no,
            "delivery_status": delivery.delivery_status,
            "order_status": order.status,
            "delivery_status_field": order.delivery_status,
            "delivered_at": now,
            "signature_hash": signature_hash,
            "warranty_card_no": delivery.warranty_card_no,
            "warranty_start_date": delivery.warranty_start_date,
            "warranty_end_date": warranty_end,
            "followup_plan_no": followup_plan_no,
            "notified_systems": ["M16-04", "M10-09", "M10-08", "M03-03"],
        }

    # ==================== 内部辅助 ====================

    async def _get_order(self, order_no: str) -> M10Order:
        """按单号取订单。"""
        order = await self.db.scalar(
            select(M10Order).where(
                and_(M10Order.order_no == order_no,
                     M10Order.is_deleted.is_(False))
            )
        )
        if order is None:
            raise BizError("1006001", "配镜订单不存在或已删除")
        return order

    async def _get_items(self, order_id: int) -> List[M10OrderItem]:
        """取未删除明细。"""
        return list((await self.db.scalars(
            select(M10OrderItem).where(
                and_(M10OrderItem.order_id == order_id,
                     M10OrderItem.is_deleted.is_(False))
            ).order_by(M10OrderItem.line_no)
        )).all())

    async def _next_delivery_seq(self, order_id: int) -> int:
        """计算本次取镜单序号（受 uk_m10_delivery_seq 保护）。"""
        current = await self.db.scalar(
            select(func.coalesce(func.max(M10Delivery.delivery_seq), 0)).where(
                M10Delivery.order_id == order_id
            )
        )
        return int(current or 0) + 1

    async def _pending_diff_amount(self, order_id: int) -> Decimal:
        """统计待处理改单差额合计。"""
        stmt = select(func.coalesce(func.sum(M10OrderAmend.diff_amount_snap), 0)).where(
            and_(
                M10OrderAmend.order_id == order_id,
                M10OrderAmend.diff_settle_status == "PENDING",
                M10OrderAmend.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
        )
        return Decimal(str(await self.db.scalar(stmt) or 0))

    def _delivered_result(self, delivery: M10Delivery, is_idempotent: bool) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """已完成交付的幂等返回。"""
        return {
            "order_no": delivery.order_no,
            "delivery_no": delivery.delivery_no,
            "delivery_status": delivery.delivery_status,
            "order_status": "DELIVERED",
            "delivery_status_field": "DELIVERED",
            "delivered_at": delivery.delivered_at,
            "signature_hash": delivery.signature_hash,
            "warranty_card_no": delivery.warranty_card_no,
            "warranty_start_date": delivery.warranty_start_date,
            "warranty_end_date": delivery.warranty_end_date,
            "followup_plan_no": delivery.followup_plan_no,
            "notified_systems": [],
            "is_idempotent": is_idempotent,
        }
```


## 7. 缓存与异步任务设计

### 7.1 Redis 缓存设计

| Key 模式 | 类型 | TTL | 说明 | 失效时机 | 容量估算 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m10:order:draft:{user_id}` | Hash | 24h | P03 开单草稿（防误关丢失） | 提交成功 / 手动清除 | 35 × 2KB = 70KB |
| `m10:order:detail:{order_no}` | String | 60s | 订单详情 JSON 缓存 | 订单任何写操作后 DEL | 首页 200 单 × 3KB = 600KB |
| `m10:order:delivery_status:{order_no}` | String | 30s | **P-14 取镜状态**（SLA 最严，P95 ≤ 80ms） | 状态迁移后 DEL | 500 × 1KB = 500KB |
| `m10:order:dashboard:{store_id}` | String | 60s | P01 看板聚合结果 | 定时任务 60s 刷新 | 门店数 × 8KB = 40KB |
| `m10:order:stat:{store}:{from}:{to}:{grp}` | String | 10min | P09 统计报表预聚合 | 定时任务每小时预热 | 20 × 30KB = 600KB |
| `m10:order:spec:{code}:{sph}:{cyl}` | String | 30min | 光度可做性校验结果 | `mdm.optical_spec.changed` 事件 | 5,000 × 200B = 1MB |
| `m10:order:hold:{order_no}` | Hash | 1h | 保管天数与费用快照（避免每次算） | 每日 01:00 任务刷新 | 100 × 500B = 50KB |
| `m10:order:lock:{order_no}` | String | 10s | 订单写操作分布式锁 | 操作完成主动释放 | 并发数 × 40B |
| `m10:idem:order:create:{key}` | String | 24h | 新建订单幂等 | TTL 到期 | 35/日 × 2KB = 70KB |
| `m10:idem:order:submit:{key}` | String | 24h | 提交订单幂等 | TTL 到期 | 35/日 × 1KB |
| `m10:idem:order:from-sales:{key}` | String | 24h | **P-13 反建幂等** | TTL 到期 | 50/日 × 2KB |
| `m10:idem:order:deliver:{key}` | String | 24h | 取镜提交幂等 | TTL 到期 | 35/日 × 1KB |
| `m10:order:holiday:{yyyy-mm-dd}` | String | 24h | 节假日日历（算工期用） | TTL 到期 | 365 × 20B = 7KB |
| `m10:code:PO:{yyyyMMdd}` | String | 3d | 订单号计数器 | TTL 到期 | 10 × 60B |
| `m10:code:QJ:{yyyyMMdd}` | String | 3d | 取镜单号计数器 | TTL 到期 | — |
| `m10:code:WK:{yyyyMMdd}` | String | 3d | 质保卡号计数器 | TTL 到期 | — |
| `m10:code:GD:{yyyyMMdd}` | String | 3d | 改单号计数器 | TTL 到期 | — |
| `m10:code:BG:{yyyyMMdd}` | String | 3d | 保管单号计数器 | TTL 到期 | — |
| `m10:order:ws:{store_id}` | Pub/Sub | — | WebSocket 看板广播频道 | 实时 | — |

**总容量估算：** 约 3.5 MB（常驻），峰值 < 10 MB。Redis 实例为 2 GB 规划，视光域占比 < 1%。

**缓存一致性策略：**

```
写操作（开单 / 提交 / 改单 / 交付 / 状态迁移）
   │
   ├─► ① 先写数据库（事务提交）
   ├─► ② 再 DEL 相关缓存键（详情 / 取镜状态 / 看板）
   └─► ③ 最后发 WebSocket 广播刷新前台页面

读操作
   │
   ├─► ① 查缓存，命中返回
   └─► ② 未命中 → 查库 → 回填缓存（TTL 由数据热度决定）

为什么不"先删缓存再写库"：
  并发下会读到旧值回填缓存，造成缓存长期脏数据。
  "先写库再删缓存"在极端并发下仍有短暂脏读，但 TTL 足够短（30~60s），
  且取镜状态这种关键数据在服务端仍会重算校验（API-24 不信前端、不信缓存）。
```

### 7.2 Celery 异步任务

```python
# app/tasks/m10/order_tasks.py
"""M10-06 配镜订单 —— Celery 异步任务。"""

import logging
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from celery import shared_task

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

MAX_RETRY = 3
RETRY_DELAY = 60


@shared_task(bind=True, max_retries=MAX_RETRY, default_retry_delay=RETRY_DELAY)
def send_ready_notice(self, order_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """到货通知：调 M03-05 发送短信 / 微信，并写 m10_order_remind。"""
    from app.services.m10.order_hold_service import OrderHoldServiceSync

    svc = OrderHoldServiceSync()
    try:
        result = svc.send_notice(
            order_no=order_no,
            remind_type="READY",
            template_code="M10_ORDER_READY",
        )
        svc.redis.publish(
            "m10:order:ws:%s" % result["store_id"],
            '{"event": "ready", "order_no": "%s"}' % order_no,
        )
        return result
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.warning("send_ready_notice failed order_no=%s err=%s", order_no, exc)
        raise self.retry(exc=exc)


@shared_task(bind=True, max_retries=MAX_RETRY, default_retry_delay=RETRY_DELAY)
def notify_downstream_delivered(self, order_no: str, delivery_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """取镜完成后的下游分发（P-15 异步部分）。"""
    from app.clients.m10 import m03_member_client, m08_optical_member_client, m16_sales_client

    payload = {"order_no": order_no, "delivery_no": delivery_no}
    notified: List[str] = []
    errors: List[str] = []

    for name, func in (
        ("M16-04", m16_sales_client.mark_delivered),
        ("M10-08", m08_optical_member_client.on_consumed),
        ("M03-03", m03_member_client.on_optical_delivered),
    ):
        try:
            func(**payload)
            notified.append(name)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            errors.append("%s: %s" % (name, exc))

    if errors and len(errors) == 3:
        raise self.retry(exc=RuntimeError("; ".join(errors)))

    logger.info("notify_downstream_delivered order_no=%s notified=%s errors=%s",
                order_no, notified, errors)
    return {"order_no": order_no, "notified": notified, "errors": errors}


@shared_task(bind=True, max_retries=MAX_RETRY)
def push_processing_callback(self, order_no: str, lab_status: str,
                             lab_job_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """加工进度回写 M16-04 API-26（失败重试 3 次后进死信）。"""
    from app.clients.m10 import m16_sales_client

    try:
        m16_sales_client.processing_callback(
            order_no=order_no, status=lab_status, lab_job_no=lab_job_no
        )
        return {"order_no": order_no, "synced": True}
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.error("push_processing_callback failed key=%s err=%s", order_no, exc)
        raise self.retry(exc=exc)


@shared_task
def sync_sales_snapshot_batch(order_nos: List[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """批量同步 M16 财务态与金额快照（对账任务调用）。"""
    from app.services.m10.order_service_sync import OrderServiceSync

    svc = OrderServiceSync()
    fixed = 0
    for order_no in order_nos:
        if svc.sync_sales_status(order_no)["changed"]:
            fixed += 1
    return {"total": len(order_nos), "fixed": fixed}


@shared_task
def export_orders(query_json: str, user_id: int, export_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """订单导出 Excel（最多 10,000 行），结果上传 OSS 供下载 24 小时。"""
    from app.services.m10.order_export_service import OrderExportService

    svc = OrderExportService()
    url = svc.export(query_json, user_id, export_id)
    logger.info("export_orders done export_id=%s url=%s", export_id, url)
    return {"export_id": export_id, "url": url}


@shared_task
def accrue_hold_fee() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """保管费快照刷新（每日 01:00）：更新 hold_days 与 hold_fee_snap。"""
    from app.services.m10.order_hold_service import OrderHoldServiceSync

    svc = OrderHoldServiceSync()
    updated = svc.accrue_all()
    return {"updated": updated}


@shared_task
def mark_remind_effective() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """标记催取有效性：发送后 3 天内取镜的置 is_effective = TRUE。"""
    from app.services.m10.order_hold_service import OrderHoldServiceSync

    svc = OrderHoldServiceSync()
    marked = svc.mark_effective(window_days=3)
    return {"marked": marked}
```

**任务清单：**

| 任务 | 触发 | 说明 | 失败处理 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `send_ready_notice` | 事件 `m10.order.ready` | 到货通知短信 / 微信 | 重试 3 次，仍失败转人工电话 |
| `notify_downstream_delivered` | P-15 完成后 | 分发 M16-04 / M10-08 / M03-03 | 全部失败才重试；部分失败仅告警 |
| `push_processing_callback` | 加工状态变化 | 回写 M16-04 API-26 | 重试 3 次后进死信队列 |
| `sync_sales_snapshot_batch` | 对账任务 | 批量修正 M16 快照 | 记录差异报告 |
| `export_orders` | 用户点击导出 | 生成 Excel | 失败通知用户重试 |
| `accrue_hold_fee` | 每日 01:00 | 保管天数与费用快照 | 幂等，可重跑 |
| `mark_remind_effective` | 每日 02:00 | 催取有效性回填 | 幂等，可重跑 |

### 7.3 APScheduler 定时任务

| 任务 ID | Cron（Asia/Shanghai） | 说明 | 幂等 | 告警 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m10_order_ready_remind` | `0 9 * * *` | 每日 09:00 扫描到货满 3 天未取，自动催取 | 是（`remind_seq` 唯一） | 失败告警 |
| `m10_order_overdue_warn` | `0 10 * * *` | 每日 10:00 超期预警（超保留期 7 天） | 是 | 超期 > 20 单告警 |
| `m10_order_hold_transfer` | `0 7 * * *` | 每日 07:00 超 `pickup_deadline` 的订单转保管（待人工确认位置） | 是（部分唯一索引） | — |
| `m10_order_hold_fee` | `0 1 * * *` | 每日 01:00 刷新保管天数与费用快照 | 是 | — |
| `m10_order_remind_effective` | `0 2 * * *` | 每日 02:00 标记催取有效性 | 是 | — |
| `m10_order_warranty_expire` | `0 9 * * *` | 每日 09:00 质保到期前 7 天提醒 | 是 | — |
| `m10_order_repair_overtime` | `*/30 * * * *` | 每 30 分钟扫描维修超 120 分钟未判定 | 是 | 超 10 单告警 |
| `m10_order_lab_overtime` | `0 9 * * *` | 每日 09:00 加工超预计到货日预警 | 是 | 超 10 单告警 |
| `m10_order_reconcile` | `10 3 * * *` | 每日 03:10 与 M16 对账（金额 / 财务态 / 销售单关联） | 是 | 差异 > 0 告警 |
| `m10_order_data_quality` | `30 3 * * *` | 每日 03:30 业务档案完整性扫描（缺光度 / 缺工序） | 是 | 缺失率 > 1% 告警 |
| `m10_order_ensure_partition` | `0 4 25 * *` | 每月 25 日 04:00 预建下月日志分区 | 是 | 失败告警 |
| `m10_order_hold_dispose_warn` | `0 8 * * *` | 每日 08:00 保管超 180 天待处置提醒 | 是 | — |

```python
# app/jobs/m10/order_jobs.py
"""M10-06 配镜订单 —— APScheduler 定时任务。"""

import logging
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any, Dict

from sqlalchemy import and_, func, select, update

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

PICKUP_REMIND_DAYS = 3
PICKUP_REMIND_MAX = 4
OVERDUE_WARN_DAYS = 7
PICKUP_KEEP_DAYS = 30
HOLD_MAX_DAYS = 180


async def ready_remind_job(db: Any, redis: Any) -> Dict[str, int]:
    """扫描到货满 N 天未取、催取次数未达上限的订单，逐单催取。

    Args:
        db: 异步会话。
        redis: Redis 客户端。

    Returns:
        统计结果 {"scanned": n, "sent": m, "skipped": k}。
    """
    from app.models.m10.order import M10Order, M10OrderRemind
    from app.tasks.m10.order_tasks import send_ready_notice

    threshold = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=PICKUP_REMIND_DAYS)
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(M10Order.order_no, M10Order.store_id).where(
            and_(
                M10Order.delivery_status.in_(["READY_TO_PICK", "NOTIFIED"]),
                M10Order.ready_at <= threshold,
                M10Order.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
        )
    )).all()

    sent, skipped = 0, 0
    for order_no, store_id in rows:
        cnt = await db.scalar(
            select(func.count()).select_from(M10OrderRemind).where(
                and_(
                    M10OrderRemind.order_no == order_no,
                    M10OrderRemind.remind_type.in_(["READY", "PICKUP_REMIND"]),
                    M10OrderRemind.is_deleted.is_(False),
                )
            )
        )
        if int(cnt or 0) >= PICKUP_REMIND_MAX:
            skipped += 1
            continue
        send_ready_notice.delay(order_no)
        await redis.publish(
            "m10:order:ws:%s" % store_id,
            '{"event": "remind", "order_no": "%s"}' % order_no,
        )
        sent += 1

    logger.info("ready_remind_job scanned=%d sent=%d skipped=%d",
                len(rows), sent, skipped)
    return {"scanned": len(rows), "sent": sent, "skipped": skipped}


async def overdue_warn_job(db: Any, redis: Any) -> Dict[str, int]:
    """超期预警：超过取镜截止日 N 天的订单发超期催告。"""
    from app.models.m10.order import M10Order
    from app.tasks.m10.order_tasks import send_ready_notice

    deadline = date.today() - timedelta(days=OVERDUE_WARN_DAYS)
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(M10Order.order_no).where(
            and_(
                M10Order.delivery_status.in_(["READY_TO_PICK", "NOTIFIED"]),
                M10Order.pickup_deadline < deadline,
                M10Order.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
        )
    )).all()

    for (order_no,) in rows:
        send_ready_notice.delay(order_no)

    await redis.set("m10:order:overdue_count", str(len(rows)), ex=86400)
    logger.info("overdue_warn_job count=%d", len(rows))
    return {"count": len(rows)}


async def hold_fee_job(db: Any, redis: Any) -> Dict[str, int]:
    """刷新保管天数与保管费快照（金额真值由 M16 结算）。"""
    from app.models.m10.order_hold import M10OrderHold

    today = date.today()
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(M10OrderHold).where(
            and_(M10OrderHold.hold_status == "HOLDING",
                 M10OrderHold.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )
    )).scalars().all()

    for hold in rows:
        hold.hold_days = max(
            (today - hold.hold_start_at.date()).days, 0
        )
        if hold.hold_fee_rule == "PER_DAY":
            billable = max(hold.hold_days - hold.free_hold_days, 0)
            hold.hold_fee_snap = round(
                float(hold.hold_fee_daily_snap) * billable, 2
            )
        elif hold.hold_fee_rule == "FREE":
            hold.hold_fee_snap = 0.0

    await db.commit()
    await redis.set("m10:order:hold_fee_refreshed_at",
                    datetime.now().isoformat(), ex=86400)
    logger.info("hold_fee_job updated=%d", len(rows))
    return {"updated": len(rows)}


async def reconcile_job(db: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """每日对账：M16 财务态 / 金额快照 / 销售单关联完整性。"""
    from app.clients.m10 import m16_sales_client
    from app.models.m10.order import M10Order

    since = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=3)
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(M10Order.order_no, M10Order.sales_order_no,
               M10Order.sales_order_status, M10Order.total_amount_snap,
               M10Order.paid_amount_snap).where(
            and_(M10Order.order_at >= since,
                 M10Order.sales_order_no.is_not(None),
                 M10Order.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )
    )).all()

    diffs = []
    for order_no, sales_no, sales_status, total_snap, paid_snap in rows:
        try:
            remote = await m16_sales_client.get_optical_order(sales_no)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            diffs.append({"order_no": order_no, "type": "FETCH_FAILED",
                          "detail": str(exc)})
            continue
        if remote.get("status") != sales_status:
            diffs.append({"order_no": order_no, "type": "STATUS_DIFF",
                          "local": sales_status, "remote": remote.get("status")})
        if abs(float(remote.get("total_amount", 0)) - float(total_snap)) > 0.01:
            diffs.append({"order_no": order_no, "type": "TOTAL_DIFF",
                          "local": float(total_snap),
                          "remote": float(remote.get("total_amount", 0))})
        if abs(float(remote.get("paid_amount", 0)) - float(paid_snap)) > 0.01:
            diffs.append({"order_no": order_no, "type": "PAID_DIFF",
                          "local": float(paid_snap),
                          "remote": float(remote.get("paid_amount", 0))})

    logger.warning("reconcile_job orders=%d diffs=%d", len(rows), len(diffs))
    return {"orders": len(rows), "diffs": len(diffs), "detail": diffs[:50]}


async def data_quality_job(db: Any) -> Dict[str, int]:
    """业务档案完整性扫描：镜片行缺光度 / 缺工序 / 缺眼别。"""
    from app.models.m10.order_item import M10OrderItem

    missing_rx = await db.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(M10OrderItem).where(
            and_(
                M10OrderItem.item_type == "LENS",
                M10OrderItem.sphere.is_(None),
                M10OrderItem.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
        )
    )
    missing_eye = await db.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(M10OrderItem).where(
            and_(
                M10OrderItem.item_type == "LENS",
                M10OrderItem.eye_side == "NA",
                M10OrderItem.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
        )
    )
    logger.warning("data_quality_job missing_rx=%s missing_eye=%s",
                   missing_rx, missing_eye)
    return {"missing_rx": int(missing_rx or 0),
            "missing_eye": int(missing_eye or 0)}


JOB_CONFIG = [
    {"id": "m10_order_ready_remind", "func": ready_remind_job,
     "cron": "0 9 * * *", "name": "到货催取"},
    {"id": "m10_order_overdue_warn", "func": overdue_warn_job,
     "cron": "0 10 * * *", "name": "超期预警"},
    {"id": "m10_order_hold_fee", "func": hold_fee_job,
     "cron": "0 1 * * *", "name": "保管费快照刷新"},
    {"id": "m10_order_reconcile", "func": reconcile_job,
     "cron": "10 3 * * *", "name": "M16 每日对账"},
    {"id": "m10_order_data_quality", "func": data_quality_job,
     "cron": "30 3 * * *", "name": "档案完整性扫描"},
]


def register_jobs(scheduler: Any, db_factory: Any, redis: Any) -> None:
    """把本子模块的定时任务注册到全局调度器。

    Args:
        scheduler: APScheduler 实例。
        db_factory: 异步会话工厂。
        redis: Redis 客户端。
    """
    for item in JOB_CONFIG:
        func = item["func"]

        async def _runner(f=func):
            async with db_factory() as db:
                return await f(db, redis)

        scheduler.add_job(
            _runner,
            trigger="cron",
            id=item["id"],
            name=item["name"],
            replace_existing=True,
            misfire_grace_time=600,
            coalesce=True,
            max_instances=1,
        )
    logger.info("m10-06 jobs registered: %d", len(JOB_CONFIG))
```

---

## 8. 业务规则与校验

### 8.1 编码规则

| 规则 | 内容 | 违反返回 |
|---|---|---|
| 订单号 | `PO` + `YYYYMMDD` + 4 位流水（`^PO[0-9]{12}$`），Redis INCR 生成，每日 03:00 对账 | `"1006003"` |
| 改单号 | `GD` + `YYYYMMDD` + 4 位（`^GD[0-9]{12}$`） | `"1006003"` |
| 取镜单号 | `QJ` + `YYYYMMDD` + 4 位（`^QJ[0-9]{12}$`） | `"1006003"` |
| 质保卡号 | `WK` + `YYYYMMDD` + 4 位（`^WK[0-9]{12}$`） | `"1006003"` |
| 保管单号 | `BG` + `YYYYMMDD` + 4 位（`^BG[0-9]{12}$`） | `"1006003"` |
| 销售单号（引用） | `SO` + `YYYYMMDD` + 3 位（`^SO[0-9]{11}$`），M16 生成 | `"1006003"`（P-13 校验） |
| 单号唯一 | 数据库唯一索引 + Redis 计数器，每日对账 `max(order_no)` 与计数器 | 告警 + 自动修正计数器 |
| 行号 | 从 1 起，单内唯一，最多 100 | `"1006018"` |
| 改单序号 | 从 1 起，单内唯一，最多 20 | `"1006041"` |
| 交付序号 | 从 1 起，单内唯一，最多 10 | `"1006038"` |
| 催取序号 | 从 1 起，单内唯一（按类型），最多 4 | `"1006042"` |

### 8.2 唯一性规则

| 对象 | 唯一键 | 实现 | 冲突处理 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 订单号 | `order_no` | `uk_m10_order_no`（部分索引 `is_deleted = FALSE`） | Redis 保证不重复；冲突则重新生成 |
| **销售单关联** | `sales_order_no` | **`uk_m10_order_sales`（部分索引）** | **幂等返回已存在档案**（核心设计） |
| 明细行 | `(order_id, line_no)` | `uk_m10_order_item_line` | `"1006018"` |
| 改单号 | `amend_no` | `uk_m10_order_amend_no` | 重新生成 |
| 改单序号 | `(order_id, amend_seq)` | `uk_m10_order_amend_seq` | `"1006041"` |
| 取镜单号 | `delivery_no` | `uk_m10_delivery_no` | 重新生成 |
| 交付序号 | `(order_id, delivery_seq)` | **`uk_m10_delivery_seq`** | **`"1006038"` 防重复交付** |
| 交付行 | `(delivery_id, order_item_id)` | `uk_m10_delivery_item_line` | `"1006038"` |
| 质保 / 维修号 | `warranty_no` | `uk_m10_warranty_no` | 重新生成 |
| 保管单号 | `hold_no` | `uk_m10_order_hold_no` | 重新生成 |
| 保管中订单 | `order_id` WHERE `hold_status = 'HOLDING'` | **`uk_m10_order_hold_order`（部分索引）** | `"1006042"` |
| 通知序号 | `(order_id, remind_type, remind_seq)` | `uk_m10_order_remind_seq` | 跳过不重复发送 |

### 8.3 状态流转规则

| 规则 | 说明 | 违反返回 |
|---|---|---|
| 业务态与财务态必须匹配 | 数据库 `ck_m10_order_status_map` 装进共享规范 13.1 映射表 | `"1006019"` |
| 不可跳态 | 必须按 `DRAFT→SUBMITTED→PAID→IN_PRODUCTION→READY→DELIVERED` 顺序 | `"1006019"` |
| 只有 `DRAFT` 可改单与删除 | 提交后改单走改单流程 | `"1006035"` |
| 只有 `PAID` 可派工 | 未付款不派工（避免加工后顾客不来） | `"1006019"` |
| 只有 `READY` 可取镜 | 其余状态一律拒绝 | `"1006020"` |
| `IN_PRODUCTION` 取消必须质检退货 | 已加工镜片有实物损失 | `"1006040"` |
| `PAID` 及以后取消需 M16 退费 | 钱已收，不能凭空取消 | `"1006027"` |
| 取消必须填原因 ≥ 10 字 | 数据库 CHECK + 应用层 | `"1006003"` |
| 退换由 M16 事件驱动 | M10 被动接收 `m16.optical.returned` | — |
| 终态不可再迁 | `CANCELLED` / `RETURNED` 只读（可补单重建） | `"1006019"` |
| 状态日志禁止 `from = to` | 数据库 `ck_m10_order_status_log_changed` | 50000（系统异常） |
| 事件驱动迁移幂等 | `source_event_id` 已存在则跳过 | 幂等返回 |

### 8.4 业务校验规则

#### 8.4.1 开单校验（P03 / API-07）

| 规则 | 说明 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| 明细非空 | 至少 1 行，最多 50 行 | `"1006003"` / `"1006018"` |
| 顾客信息完整 | 姓名非空 + 手机号 11 位 | `"1006010"` |
| 光度可做性 | 调 M01-08 `specs/lookup` | `"1006004"` |
| 球柱镜 0.25D 步长 | `sphere × 4` 为整数 | `"1006011"` |
| 柱镜为 0 不可填轴位 | — | `"1006012"` |
| 柱镜非 0 必须填轴位 | — | `"1006013"` |
| 轴位 1~180 | — | `"1006012"` |
| 瞳距和校验 | 单眼和与总瞳距差 ≤ 1.00mm | `"1006014"` |
| 瞳距范围 | 总 40~80mm，单眼 20~45mm | `"1006015"` |
| 渐进片必填近附加与瞳高 | — | `"1006016"` |
| 棱镜类必填棱镜度与底向 | — | `"1006003"` |
| 镜片数量与眼别一致 | OU=2，OD/OS=1 | `"1006017"` |
| 镜片行眼别不能是 NA | — | `"1006017"` |
| 商品可售 | M01-08 状态 ON_SALE | `"1006002"` |
| 验光单有效 | 存在、已签发、未作废、未过期 | `"1006008"` |
| 处方有效 | `is_prescription` 商品必须有 AUDITED 且未过期处方 | `"1006009"` |
| 补单指向原单 | 原单存在、已交付、在有效期内 | `"1006044"` |

#### 8.4.2 取镜四道校验（P05 / API-23 / API-24）—— 本子模块核心

**四道校验逐条对应关系：**

| 道次 | 校验名 | 判定逻辑 | 通过条件 | 失败错误码 | 失败提示 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **第一道** | **订单已付清** | `sales_order_status IN ('PAID','DELIVERED')` **或** `paid_amount_snap >= total_amount_snap` | 财务态以 M16 为准，快照兜底 | `"1006021"` | 订单尚未付清，请到收银台补缴尾款 |
| **第二道** | **加工已完工** | `lab_status = 'COMPLETED'` **或** `need_processing = FALSE` | 加工单质检通过 | `"1006022"` | 加工尚未完工，暂不可取镜 |
| **第三道** | **全部到货** | 所有明细 `arrive_status IN ('ARRIVED','NOT_REQUIRED')` | 无 `PENDING`/`PARTIAL` 行 | `"1006023"` | 存在未到货商品，暂不可取镜 |
| **第四道** | **顾客信息确认** | 6 项确认项全勾 + 逐行参数核对完成 | `check_customer_confirm = TRUE` | `"1006024"` | 请先完成顾客信息与商品确认项 |

**第一道为什么以 M16 财务态为最终依据？**
`paid_amount_snap` 是**快照**，由事件推送更新，存在秒级延迟（事件队列积压时可能落后几十秒）。
顾客刚在收银台付完尾款就走到取镜台，若只看快照会误判「未付清」。因此第一道**优先读
`sales_order_status`**（同样由事件更新但语义更明确），再做一次 M16-04 API-25 兜底查询（超时 1s
则降级用快照 + 提示人工核对）。

**四道校验的短路顺序与幂等：**

```
check_paid  ──失败──► 返回 1006021（不继续检查后续）
   │通过
   ▼
check_lab_done ──失败──► 返回 1006022
   │通过
   ▼
check_all_arrived ──失败──► 返回 1006023（附未到货行清单）
   │通过
   ▼
check_customer_confirm ──失败──► 返回 1006024（附确认项清单）
   │通过
   ▼
四道全通过 → 生成取镜单
```

> **短路的意义：** 只返回第一个卡点，前台一次只需处理一个问题（如先收钱再谈其他），避免一次抛出
> 4 个错误让顾客困惑。完整四道状态仍在响应体中返回，供页面展示进度。

**第四道顾客确认项清单（6 项必勾）：**

| # | key | 文案 | 作用 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `rx_matched` | 已核对镜片度数与订单一致 | 度数纠纷举证 |
| 2 | `frame_ok` | 已核对镜框外观无瑕疵 | 外观纠纷举证 |
| 3 | `wear_ok` | 已确认佩戴舒适、无头晕 | 佩戴不适纠纷举证 |
| 4 | `care_guide` | 已告知镜片清洁与保养要点 | 使用不当纠纷举证 |
| 5 | `warranty` | 已告知质保范围与期限 | 质保纠纷举证 |
| 6 | `followup` | 已告知复查时间 | 复查依从性 |

**交付举证材料（与四道校验配套）：**

| 举证项 | 字段 | 是否强制 | 作用 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 顾客签字 | `sign_type` + `signature_url` + `signature_hash` | **强制** | 证明「顾客确实来取过」 |
| 镜片包装拍照 | `photo_package_url` | **强制**（`M10.ORDER.PHOTO_REQUIRED`） | 证明「交付时参数标签核对过」 |
| 成品眼镜拍照 | `photo_product_url` | 建议 | 证明「交付时外观完好」 |
| 取镜人证件拍照 | `photo_id_card_url` | 代取时强制 | 证明「取镜人身份」 |
| 逐行参数核对 | `m10_delivery_item.param_matched` + `param_diff` | **强制** | 证明「每片都核对过」 |
| 防伪码 | `serial_no` | 镜片行强制 | 质保反查与召回追溯 |
| 签字哈希 | `signature_hash`（SHA-256） | 自动计算 | 防篡改 |
| 照片组哈希 | `photo_hash`（三张拼接后 SHA-256） | 自动计算 | 防替换 |

**举证材料的法律意义：**

```
纠纷场景一：「你们给我的度数不对」
  → M10 举证：param_matched = TRUE 且 param_diff 为空 且 serial_no 已录
  → 若 param_matched = FALSE：说明现场发现了差异并按 exception_action 处理
    （REJECT 拒收 / ACCEPT_WITH_DISCOUNT 折扣接受），责任已当场划分

纠纷场景二：「我根本没取过这副眼镜」
  → M10 举证：签名图片 + SHA-256 + signed_at + delivered_by + 照片
  → 哈希保证签名文件事后未被替换

纠纷场景三：「你们把我眼镜弄丢了」
  → M10 举证：m10_order_hold.remind_count（催告次数）+ last_remind_at +
    remind_confirmed（顾客是否回应）+ liability_basis
  → 催告不足 3 次，医院责任权重上升

纠纷场景四：「镜片脱膜要免费换」
  → M10 举证：m10_warranty.in_warranty（在保判定）+ judge_basis（判定依据 ≥10 字）+
    fault_images（故障照片）
```

#### 8.4.3 改单校验（P04 / API-18）

| 规则 | 说明 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| 订单非终态 | `CANCELLED` / `RETURNED` 不可改 | `"1006035"` |
| 原因 ≥ 10 字 | — | `"1006036"` |
| 改单次数 ≤ 20 | 防反复修改 | `"1006041"` |
| 至少 1 项变更 | `changes` 非空 | `"1006003"` |
| ADD 必须带明细 | — | `"1006003"` |
| UPDATE/REMOVE 必须带行号 | — | `"1006003"` |
| 加工中改单必须确认返工 | `accept_rework = true` 且 `rework_confirm_no` 非空 | `"1006040"` |
| 加工中改单必须审批 | `need_approval = TRUE` | `"1006039"` |
| 差额 ≥ 500 需审批 | `M10.ORDER.AMEND_DIFF_APPROVAL` | `"1006039"` |
| 差额未结清阻断交付 | `diff_settle_status <> 'PENDING'` | `"1006037"` |
| 差额等式 | DB `ck_m10_order_amend_diff` | 50000 |
| 改后总额由 M16 试算 | 前端不可输入 | `"1006005"` |

**改单与加工影响的强约束：**

```
加工中改单（amend_stage = IN_PRODUCTION）：
  ① 必须勾选「接受返工」并填写质检退货单号（rework_confirm_no）
  ② 必须提交审批（need_approval = TRUE）
  ③ 审批通过后才真正执行，执行时会：
     - 调 M10-07 作废原加工单（已加工镜片报废 SCRAP）
     - 重新派工生成新加工单（lab_job_no_new）
     - 生成改单记录，lab_impact 标记为 SCRAP / REWORK / REASSIGN
  ④ 未通过审批前，订单明细保持不变（before_snapshot 可回滚）
```

#### 8.4.4 质保与维修校验（P06）

| 规则 | 说明 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| 订单必须已交付 | `status = 'DELIVERED'` | `"1006030"` |
| 质保记录存在 | 有 `WARRANTY_CARD` 行 | `"1006043"` |
| 故障描述 ≥ 5 字 | — | `"1006033"` |
| 判定依据 ≥ 10 字 | 核心举证字段 | `"1006034"` |
| 免费必须真在保 | DB `ck_m10_warranty_in_warranty` | `"1006045"` |
| 付费必须有收费项目 | — | `"1006048"` |
| 免费换新需审批 | `need_approval = TRUE` | `"1006039"` |
| 维修完成必须有技师与时间 | — | `"1006049"` |
| 维修后必须重新交付 | 生成第二张取镜单，重走四道校验 | `"1006022"`（若未完工） |

#### 8.4.5 催取与超期校验（P07）

| 规则 | 说明 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| 催取次数 ≤ 4 | `M10.ORDER.PICKUP_REMIND_MAX` | `"1006042"` |
| 同一序号不重复发送 | `uk_m10_order_remind_seq` | 幂等跳过 |
| 已超期才可转保管 | `pickup_deadline < CURRENT_DATE` | `"1006039"` |
| 保管位置必填 | — | `"1006003"` |
| 一个订单只能一条保管中 | `uk_m10_order_hold_order` | `"1006042"` |
| 按日计费必须配置费率 | `hold_fee_daily > 0` | `"40001"` |
| 遗失必须责任 + 依据 + 审批 | DB `ck_m10_order_hold_lost` | `"1006034"` |
| 免费规则不得产生费用 | DB `ck_m10_order_hold_free` | `"1006045"` |

### 8.5 计算规则

| 计算项 | 公式 | 精度 / 舍入 | 权威方 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 应收总额 | **M16-01 / M16-04 计算**，M10 只存 `total_amount_snap` | `ROUND_HALF_UP` 到分 | **M16** |
| 优惠金额 | **M16-06 计算** | 到分 | **M16** |
| 预计到货日 | `订单日期 + MAX(processing_days)`，工作日口径，加急 × 0.6 | 整数天 | M10 |
| 取镜截止日 | `ready_at::date + 30 天` | 整数天 | M10 |
| 到货天数 | `CURRENT_DATE - ready_at::date` | 整数天 | M10 |
| 超期天数 | `MAX(CURRENT_DATE - pickup_deadline, 0)` | 整数天 | M10 |
| 质保到期日 | `delivered_at::date + N 个月`（**月末对齐**） | 月 | M10（月数来自 M01-08） |
| 质保剩余天数 | `warranty_end_date - CURRENT_DATE` | 整数天 | M10 |
| 改单差额 | `after_amount_snap - before_amount_snap` | 到分，DB CHECK 强制等式 | M16 试算，M10 记录 |
| 保管天数 | `COALESCE(hold_end_at, NOW())::date - hold_start_at::date` | 整数天 | M10 |
| 保管费 | `ROUND(日费率 × MAX(保管天数 - 免费天数, 0), 2)` | 到分 | M10 算快照，**M16 结算** |
| 交付完成判定 | 全部明细 `is_delivered = TRUE` | 布尔 | M10 |
| 参数不符率 | `COUNT(param_matched = FALSE) / COUNT(*)` | 4 位小数（%） | M10 |
| 按期交付率 | `COUNT(delivered_at::date <= promised_ready_date) / COUNT(delivered)` | 4 位小数 | M10 |

**质保月末对齐示例：**

| 交付日 | 质保月数 | 到期日 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-10-26 | 12 | 2027-10-26 | 常规 |
| 2026-01-31 | 1 | 2026-02-28 | 月末对齐（2026 非闰年） |
| 2028-01-31 | 1 | 2028-02-29 | 月末对齐（2028 闰年） |
| 2026-08-31 | 6 | 2027-02-28 | 月末对齐 |
| 2026-11-04 | 6 | 2027-05-04 | 常规 |

### 8.6 引用与删除规则

| 规则 | 内容 | 违反处理 |
|---|---|---|
| 逻辑删除 | 一律 `is_deleted = TRUE`，**禁止物理删除** | 代码审查拦截 |
| 订单删除 | 仅 `DRAFT` 且无销售单、无取镜单、无质保记录可删除 | `"1006019"` |
| 明细删除 | 随订单逻辑删除；已交付明细永不删除 | — |
| 状态日志 | **只增不改不删**，永不删除（审计要求） | 数据库权限限制（无 DELETE 授权） |
| 取镜单 | 作废改写 `delivery_status = 'CANCELLED'`，不删除 | — |
| 质保记录 | 永不删除（售后追溯期 10 年） | 数据库权限限制 |
| 通知记录 | 按分区归档，不删除在线数据 | — |
| 保管记录 | 永不删除（法律责任追溯） | 数据库权限限制 |
| 引用上游 | `sales_order_no` / `patient_id` 等只引用不删除，悬挂引用由对账告警 | 每日 `reconcile_job` |
| 商品下架 | 已开单明细保留快照（`product_name` / `brand`），不受 M01-08 下架影响 | — |

### 8.7 并发控制

| 场景 | 机制 | 冲突返回 |
|---|---|---|
| 订单修改 / 改单 / 取镜提交 | 乐观锁 `version` 字段（`WHERE version = :v`） | `40900` |
| 两条创建路径并发 | **唯一索引 `uk_m10_order_sales` + 捕获 `23505` 转幂等返回** | 幂等返回原档案 |
| 重复交付 | 唯一索引 `uk_m10_delivery_seq`（`order_id, delivery_seq`） | `"1006038"` |
| 保管单重复 | 部分唯一索引 `uk_m10_order_hold_order`（`WHERE hold_status = 'HOLDING'`） | `"1006042"` |
| 催取重复发送 | 唯一索引 `uk_m10_order_remind_seq`（`order_id, remind_type, remind_seq`） | 幂等跳过 |
| 派工重复 | 检查 `lab_job_no` 是否已存在且未取消 | 幂等返回原单 |
| 拣选订单行（定时任务） | `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` | 跳过已被其他实例处理的行 |
| P-15 重复通知 | 按 `delivery_no` 幂等 + `is_idempotent` 标记 | 幂等返回 |
| 金额快照更新 | 事件按 `occurred_at` 比较，旧事件丢弃 | 静默丢弃 + 日志 |
| 事务隔离 | `READ COMMITTED`（PostgreSQL 默认） | — |

**并发幂等的实现要点（P-13 反建）：**

```python
# 两条路径并发时的唯一正确写法
try:
    self.db.add(order)
    await self.db.flush()          # 触发唯一索引检查
except IntegrityError as exc:
    await self.db.rollback()
    if "uk_m10_order_sales" in str(exc.orig):
        existed = await self.db.scalar(
            select(M10Order).where(M10Order.sales_order_no == sales_order_no)
        )
        if existed:
            return {"order_no": existed.order_no, "is_idempotent": True}
    raise
```

> **为什么不能只靠「先查后写」？** 两个请求同时「查 → 都没查到 → 都插入」，在 `READ COMMITTED`
> 隔离级别下会产生两条档案。唯一索引是最底层、最可靠的防线，应用层的查只是优化（减少异常开销）。

---

## 9. 权限与安全

### 9.1 功能权限

| 权限编码 | 名称 | 说明 | 授予角色 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `m10:order:view` | 查看 | 订单列表、详情、时间轴、取镜单查询 | 全部视光角色、财务 |
| `m10:order:create` | 开单 | 新建、修改草稿、提交、取消、派工、补单、催加工 | 销售员、视光师、主管 |
| `m10:order:update` | 更新 | 同步财务态、回填差额结算 | 主管、系统 |
| `m10:order:amend` | 改单 | 改单试算与提交 | 销售员、主管 |
| `m10:order:approve` | 审批 | 受限改单审批、免费换新审批、保管费减免、遗失处置 | 主管、店长 |
| `m10:order:deliver` | 取镜交付 | 四道校验、创建取镜单、签字、完成交付 | 取镜专员、收银员 |
| `m10:order:warranty` | 质保维修 | 维修受理、质保判定、维修完成 | 售后专员、主管 |
| `m10:order:remind` | 催取 | 待催取清单、发送催取通知 | 客服、取镜专员、主管 |
| `m10:order:hold` | 超期保管 | 转保管登记、遗失判定、保管清单 | 主管、售后专员 |
| `m10:order:print` | 打印 | 订单凭条、取镜单、质保卡、加工委托单 | 销售员、取镜专员、财务 |
| `m10:order:export` | 导出 | 订单列表导出 Excel | 主管、财务 |
| `m10:order:stat` | 统计 | 业务统计报表与导出 | 主管、店长、财务 |
| `m10:order:config` | 配置 | 保留天数、催取策略、保管费率等参数 | 店长、系统管理员 |

### 9.2 权限矩阵

| 角色 | view | create | update | amend | approve | deliver | warranty | remind | hold | print | export | stat | config |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **视光销售员** | ● | ● | ○ | ● | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ | ● | ○ | ○ | ○ |

| **视光收银员** | ● | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ | ● | ○ | ○ | ○ | ● | ○ | ○ | ○ |
| **取镜专员（前台）** | ● | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ | ● | ○ | ● | ○ | ● | ○ | ○ | ○ |
| **加工师** | ● | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ | △ | ○ | ○ | ○ |
| **售后专员** | ● | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ○ | ○ | ○ |
| **客服** | ● | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ | ● | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ | ○ |
| **视光主管** | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ○ |

> ● 有权限（数据权限内）；○ 无权限；△ 仅查看加工委托单。
> **审批权限（approve）不授予一线角色**：受限改单、免费换新都会产生真实成本，必须主管以上。

### 9.3 数据权限

| 角色 | 数据范围 |
|---|---|
| 视光销售员 / 视光师 | **仅本人开的单**（`seller_id = self`），可查看本店待取镜（只读） |
| 视光收银员 / 取镜专员 | **本店全部**（`store_id = self_store`） |
| 加工师 | 本店 `IN_PRODUCTION` 及以后状态的订单（只读） |
| 售后专员 / 客服 | 本店全部 |
| 视光主管 | **本店全部**（含审批） |
| 店长 | **本店全部**（含配置） |
| 财务 | **全院**（只读，金额快照仅供业务参考） |
| 系统 / 内部服务 | 按调用方授权范围（P-12 等契约按 `service_name` 白名单） |

**脱敏规则：**

| 字段 | 销售员 | 取镜专员 | 售后 / 客服 | 财务 | 内部契约 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 顾客姓名 | 全名 | 全名 | 全名 | 全名 | 全名 |
| 手机号 | `138****1234` | **全号**（需联系顾客） | **全号** | `138****1234` | `138****1234` |
| 取镜人证件号 | 不可见 | 后 4 位 | 后 4 位 | 不可见 | 不返回 |
| 签名图片地址 | 不可见 | 可见（签名 URL） | 可见 | 不可见 | 不返回 |
| 交付照片地址 | 不可见 | 可见（签名 URL） | 可见 | 不可见 | 不返回 |
| 故障照片 | 不可见 | 不可见 | 可见（签名 URL） | 不可见 | 不返回 |
| 内部备注 | 可见 | 可见 | 可见 | **不可见** | 不返回 |
| 成本 / 毛利 | 全部不可见 | 不可见 | 不可见 | 可见（走 M16/M22） | 不返回 |

**多门店隔离：**

```python
# app/core/data_scope.py（示意）
"""M10-06 数据权限过滤。"""


def apply_order_scope(stmt, user):
    """按角色给订单查询追加数据范围条件。"""
    if user.has_role("FINANCE") or user.has_role("ADMIN"):
        return stmt
    if user.has_role("SALES") or user.has_role("OPTOMETRIST"):
        return stmt.where(M10Order.seller_id == user.id)
    if user.store_ids:
        return stmt.where(M10Order.store_id.in_(user.store_ids))
    return stmt.where(M10Order.store_id == -1)
```

### 9.4 安全措施

| 措施 | 说明 |
|---|---|
| **签名字段防篡改** | `signature_hash` = 签名图片 SHA-256；任何替换文件都会导致哈希不匹配，可在纠纷时校验 |
| **照片组防篡改** | `photo_hash` = 三张照片地址拼接后 SHA-256，整组举证材料不可替换 |
| **二维码防伪** | 单号 + HMAC-SHA256 前 8 位（密钥在配置中心，轮换周期 90 天） |
| **手机号加密存储** | `customer_phone_enc` 用 AES-256-GCM 加密（密钥在 KMS），明文仅在有权限的接口临时解密 |
| **通知流水脱敏** | `m10_order_remind.notify_target` 只存 `139****5678`，明文不落库 |
| **证件号加密** | `pickup_person_id_card` 加密存储，界面按角色显示后 4 位 |
| **签名 URL 时效** | OSS 私有读，签名 URL 有效期 15 分钟，不可枚举 |
| **全量审计** | 所有写操作写 M02-06 审计（开单 / 改单 / 取消 / 交付 / 判定 / 遗失 / 免单） |
| **金额越界防护** | M10 无任何写金额的接口；`_snap` 字段只由事件与内部契约更新 |
| **内部契约鉴权** | 服务间 token + IP 白名单 + `service_name` 白名单；P-12~P-15 不可公网访问 |
| **批量操作限流** | 催取 ≤ 100 单/次、批打 ≤ 200 单/次、导出 ≤ 10,000 行/次 |
| **敏感操作二次确认** | 取消订单、遗失判定、免费换新需二次确认弹窗 + 审批单号 |
| **数据留存** | 订单 / 取镜单 / 质保记录永久保留；状态日志与通知 24 个月在线 + 冷存 10 年 |
| **防重复提交** | `Idempotency-Key` + 按钮禁用 + 唯一索引三重防护 |
| **SQL 注入防护** | SQLAlchemy 参数化 + 排序列白名单（`order_no` / `order_at` / `total_amount_snap` / `ready_at`） |

#### 10.1.1 新增契约（M10 首次定义）

| 编号 | 接口 | 提供方 | 消费方 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| U-20 | `POST /internal/v1/m10/lab/jobs/{lab_job_no}/urge` | M10-07 | M10-06 | 催工请求：`{"urge_reason": "...", "operator_id": 5001}` → `{"urged": true, "notified_staff": [6001]}` |
| U-26 | `POST /internal/v1/m20/approval/apply` | M20 | M10-06 / M10-07 | 通用审批发起：`{"biz_type": "M10_ORDER_AMEND", "biz_no": "GD...", "amount": 400.00, "reason": "...", "applicant_id": 5001}` → `{"approval_no": "SP..."}` |

> 上述两个接口在 M10 内首次定义，已在本子模块登记；M10-07 / M20 的后续修订需保持路径与载荷兼容。

#### 10.1.2 契约对齐说明

| # | 差异点 | 本子模块处理 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | M16-04 U-04 写作 `GET /internal/v1/m10/optometry/{no}`，本模块统一以 `/internal/v1/m10/...` 为前缀 | 引用时按 M10 共享规范 §8 P-01 路径调用，不重复实现 |
| 2 | M16-04 事件载荷中 `order_no` 指**销售单号**（`SO...`），M10 的 `order_no` 指**配镜订单号**（`PO...`） | 消费 M16 事件时一律把 `order_no` 映射到 `M10Order.sales_order_no` 字段过滤，绝不误用 `M10Order.order_no` |
| 3 | M16-04 API-26 的 `status` 枚举为 `PENDING/PROCESSING/OUTSOURCING/READY/DELIVERED/NONE` | M10-07 → M10-06 的 `lab_status` 枚举（9 态）在回写 M16 时做映射：`QC_PENDING/QC_FAILED → PROCESSING`，`COMPLETED → READY`，`NOT_STARTED → NONE` |
| 4 | M16-04 没有「光度」字段 | 从 M16 反建（P-13）时 `sphere/cylinder/axis` 允许为空，由 M10 侧补录；缺光度不影响收费，但取镜时提示 |
| 5 | M10-07 的 `m10_lab_job` 归属 M10-07 | 本子模块只存 `lab_job_no` 引用，不建表、不写状态 |

### 10.2 提供接口

| 编号 | 方法 | 接口 | 调用方 | 用途 | SLA |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **P-12** | GET | `/internal/v1/m10/orders/{order_no}` | M16-04、M22、M17 | 取配镜订单业务档案（光度 / 工序 / 交付 / 质保 / 维修） | **P95 ≤ 100ms** |
| **P-13** | POST | `/internal/v1/m10/orders/from-sales-order` | M16-04 | 由销售单反建业务档案（幂等） | **P95 ≤ 300ms** |
| **P-14** | GET | `/internal/v1/m10/orders/delivery-status/{order_no}` | M16-04、M03-05 | 取镜状态查询（四道校验卡点） | **P95 ≤ 80ms** |
| **P-15** | POST | `/internal/v1/m10/orders/on-delivered` | M16-04、M03-03、M19 | 取镜完成通知（幂等分发） | **P95 ≤ 100ms** |

**四个契约的要点对照：**

| 契约 | 幂等 | 上游超时 | 重试 | 关键字段 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| P-12 | 只读 | 500ms | 不重试（查询失败由调用方重查） | `order_no`、`status`、`items[].sphere`、`warranty_end_date` |
| P-13 | **三道防线**（Idem Key + 唯一索引 + 23505 转幂等） | 1s | 3 次指数退避 | `sales_order_no`、`is_idempotent` |
| P-14 | 只读 | 300ms | 不重试 | `can_pick`、`blocking_code`、`next_action_text` |
| P-15 | **按 `delivery_no` 幂等** | 500ms | 3 次 | `delivery_no`、`is_idempotent`、`notified_systems` |

### 10.3 事件发布

| 事件名 | 触发时机 | 载荷字段 | 订阅方 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `m10.order.created` | 订单创建（两条路径均发） | `order_no`、`sales_order_no`、`create_path`、`patient_id`、`total_amount_snap`、`item_count` | M16-04、M10-07 |
| `m10.order.submitted` | 订单提交成功 | `order_no`、`sales_order_no`、`expected_ready_date` | M16-04 |
| `m10.order.paid` | 收到 M16 付款事件后同步完成 | `order_no`、`paid_amount_snap`、`sales_order_status` | M10-07 |
| `m10.order.dispatched` | 派工成功 | `order_no`、`lab_job_no`、`lab_priority` | M16-04、M10-07 |
| `m10.order.ready` | 加工完成且全部到货 | `order_no`、`ready_at`、`pickup_deadline`、`lab_job_no` | M03-05、M16-04 |
| `m10.order.amended` | 改单提交 | `order_no`、`amend_no`、`diff_amount_snap`、`lab_impact` | M16-04、M10-07 |
| `m10.order.delivered` | 取镜交付完成 | `order_no`、`delivery_no`、`delivered_at`、`warranty_card_no`、`warranty_end_date` | M16-04、M10-08、M03-03、M19、M10-09 |
| `m10.order.cancelled` | 订单取消 | `order_no`、`cancel_type`、`reason` | M16-04、M10-07 |
| `m10.order.hold_transferred` | 超期转保管 | `order_no`、`hold_no`、`hold_location` | M22 |
| `m10.order.lost` | 保管遗失 | `order_no`、`hold_no`、`liability_result`、`compensation_amount_snap` | M22、M02 |
| `m10.delivery.reminded` | 发出催取 / 到货通知 | `order_no`、`remind_type`、`remind_seq`、`notify_channel` | M22 |
| `m10.warranty.repair.created` | 维修受理 | `warranty_no`、`order_no`、`fault_type`、`in_warranty` | M22 |
| `m10.warranty.judged` | 质保判定完成 | `warranty_no`、`judge_result`、`is_free` | M16-04、M22 |
| `m10.warranty.repaired` | 维修完成并重新交付 | `warranty_no`、`redeliver_no`、`repair_days` | M10-09、M22 |

### 10.4 事件订阅

| 事件名 | 来源 | 处理动作 | 幂等键 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `m16.optical.order.created` | M16-04 | **反建 M10 业务档案**（等价于调 P-13） | `sales_order_no` + `source_event_id` |
| `m16.optical.paid` | M16-04 | 更新金额快照与财务态；`paid_amount_snap > 0` 时业务态 `SUBMITTED → PAID` | `source_event_id` |
| `m16.optical.processing.changed` | M16-04 | 同步加工进度快照（与 M10-07 事件二选一） | `source_event_id` |
| `m16.optical.ready` | M16-04 | 更新到货状态并触发催取 | `source_event_id` |
| `m16.optical.delivered` | M16-04 | 更新取镜状态（与 P-15 二选一，幂等） | `delivery_no` |
| `m16.optical.returned` | M16-04 | 退换回写：订单 `DELIVERED → RETURNED`，写 `return_no` | `source_event_id` |
| `m16.optical.cancelled` | M16-04 | 同步财务态为 `CANCELLED` | `source_event_id` |
| `m10.lab.job.completed` | M10-07 | **加工完成**：`lab_status = COMPLETED`、订单 `IN_PRODUCTION → READY`、计算 `pickup_deadline`、发到货通知 | `lab_job_no` |
| `m10.lab.job.status_changed` | M10-07 | 同步 `lab_status` 快照并转发 M16-04 API-26 | `lab_job_no + occurred_at` |
| `m10.lab.quality.failed` | M10-07 | 质检不合格：`lab_status = QC_FAILED`，订单保持 `IN_PRODUCTION`，告警 | `lab_job_no + occurred_at` |
| `mdm.optical_product.updated` | M01-08 | 失效 `m10:order:spec:*` 与商品配置缓存 | `product_code` |
| `mdm.optical_spec.changed` | M01-08 | 失效光度缓存 | `product_code` |
| `m03.patient.merged` | M03-02 | `patient_id` 重定向（更新 `m10_order.patient_id`） | `merge_id` |
| `m20.approval.completed` | M20 | 改单审批结果回调（等价于调 API-20） | `approval_no` |
| `billing.paid` | M16-01 | 触发 `sync_sales_status` 拉取最新财务态 | `settlement_no` |
